pūjayāmāsuḥ brahmāṇam iva vāsavaḥ teṣām sa pūjām pūjā arhaḥ pratigṛhya tapodhanaḥ yathārham dānavaiḥ sārdham āsīnaḥ dānava adhipaḥ āsīnam nāradam prekṣya mayaḥ tu atha mahā asuraḥ abravīt vacanam tuṣṭaḥ hṛṣṭa roma ānana īkṣaṇaḥ dṛśyante bhaya dāḥ svapnāḥ bhajyante ca dhvajāḥ param aṭṭālakāḥ ca nṛtyante sa patākāḥ sa gopurāḥ hiṃsa hiṃsa iti śrūyante giraḥ ca bhaya dāḥ pure na aham bibhemi devānām sa indrāṇām api nārada muktvā ekam vara dam sthāṇum bhakta abhaya karam haram anāgatam atītam ca bhavān jānāti tattvataḥ nāradaḥ uvāca śṛṇu dānava tattvena bhavanti autpātikāḥ yathā utpathāt mārgam āgacchet mārgāt ca iva vimārga tām apakāriṣu devānām kuruṣe tvam sahāya tām tat etāni evamādīni utpātāḥ veditāni ca vaināśikāni dṛśyante dānavānām tathā eva ca eṣa rudraḥ samāsthāya mahā loka mayam ratham āyāti tripuram hantum maya tvām asurān api dānavānām punar devaḥ deveśa padam āgataḥ śūra saṃmatam iti evam dānavān āha dānavaḥ śūrāḥ stha jāta putrāḥ stha kṛtakṛtyāḥ stha dānavāḥ jitvā vayam bhaviṣyāmaḥ sarve amara sabhā sadaḥ devān ca sa indrakān hatvā lokān bhokṣyāmahe asurāḥ aṭṭālakeṣu ca tathā tiṣṭhadhvam śastra pāṇayaḥ daṃśitāḥ yuddha sajjāḥ ca tiṣṭhadhvam prodyata āyudhāḥ purāṇi trīṇi ca etāni yathā sthāneṣu dānavāḥ tiṣṭhadhvam laṅghanīyāni bhaviṣyanti purāṇi ca namaḥ gatāḥ tathā śūrāḥ devatāḥ viditāḥ hi vaḥ tāḥ prayatnena vāryāḥ ca vidāryāḥ ca eva sāyakaiḥ iti danu tanayān mayaḥ tathā uktvā sura gaṇa vāraṇa vāraṇe vacāṃsi yuvati jana viṣaṇṇa mānasam tat tripura puram sahasā viveśa rājā tad abhimatam adāt tatas śaśāṅkī sa ca kila nirbhayaḥ eva dānavaḥ abhūt Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca ilāvṛtam iti khyātam tat varṣam vistṛta āyatam devānām yatra vṛttāni kanyā dānāni yāni ca reme nityam bhavaḥ yatra sahāyaiḥ pārṣadaiḥ gaṇaiḥ madhu piṅgala netraḥ tu candra avayava bhūṣaṇaḥ devānām adhipam prāha gaṇapān ca maheśvaraḥ vāsava etat arīṇām te tripuram paridṛśyate idam vṛttam idam khyātam vahni vat bhṛśa tāpanam ete janāḥ giri prakhyāḥ sa kuṇḍala kirīṭinaḥ prākāra gopura aṭṭeṣu kakṣā ante dānavāḥ sthitāḥ ime ca toyada ābhāsāḥ danujāḥ vikṛta ānanāḥ nirgacchanti puras daityāḥ sa āyudhāḥ vijaya eṣiṇaḥ sa tvam sura śataiḥ sārdham sa sahāyaḥ vara āyudhaḥ puras purasya randhra arthī sthāsyāmi vijayāya vaḥ prakrānta ratha bhīmaiḥ taiḥ sa devaiḥ pārṣadām gaṇaiḥ tena nādena tripurāt dānavāḥ yuddha lālasāḥ utpatya dudruvuḥ celuḥ sa āyudhāḥ khe gaṇeśvarān anye payodhara ārāvāḥ payodhara samāḥ babhuḥ devānām siṃha nādaḥ ca sarva tūrya ravaḥ mahān candra udayāt samudbhūtaḥ paurṇamāsaḥ iva arṇavaḥ aṭṭālakān samāruhya kecid calita vādinaḥ svarṇa mālā dharāḥ śūrāḥ prabhā sitakara ambarāḥ itas ca itas ca dhāvantaḥ kecid udbhūta vāsasaḥ kim etat iti papracchuḥ anyonyam gṛham āśritāḥ jñāsyase anantareṇa iti kālaḥ vistārataḥ mahān saḥ api asau pṛthvī sāram ca siṃhaḥ ca ratham āsthitaḥ tiṣṭhate tripuram pīḍya deham vyādhiḥ iva ucchritaḥ ehi āyudham ādāya kva me pṛcchā bhaviṣyati iti te anyonyam āviddhāḥ uttara uttara bhāṣiṇaḥ āsādya pṛcchanti tadā dānavāḥ tripura ālayāḥ tāraka ākhya pure daityāḥ tāraka ākhya puraḥsarāḥ nirgatāḥ kupitāḥ tūrṇam bilāt iva mahā uragāḥ niruddhāḥ gaja rājānaḥ yathā kesari yūthapaiḥ darpitānām tatas ca eṣām darpitānām iva agnīnām rūpāṇi jajvaluḥ teṣām agnīnām iva dhamyatām tatas bṛhanti cāpāni bhīma nādāni sarvaśas nikṛṣya jaghnuḥ anyonyam iṣubhiḥ prāṇa bhojanaiḥ mārjāra mṛga bhīma āsyān pārṣadān vikṛta ānanān dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā ahasan uccais dānavāḥ rūpa sampadā bāhubhiḥ parigha ākāraiḥ kṛṣyatām dhanuṣām śarāḥ viviśuḥ taḍāgāni iva pakṣiṇaḥ mṛtāḥ stha kva nu yāsyadhvam haniṣyāmaḥ nivartatām iti evam paruṣāṇi uktvā dānavāḥ pārṣada ṛṣabhān bibhiduḥ sāyakaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ sūrya pādāḥ iva ambudān pramathāḥ api siṃha akṣāḥ siṃha vikrānta vikramāḥ khaṇḍa śaila śilā vṛkṣaiḥ bibhiduḥ daitya dānavān vikṛṣṭa cāpāḥ daitya indrāḥ sṛjanti śara durdinam iṣubhiḥ tāḍyamānāḥ te bhūyas bhūyas gaṇeśvarāḥ cakruḥ te deha niryāsam svarṇa dhātum iva acalāḥ tathā vṛkṣa śilā vajra śūla paṭṭi paraśvadhaiḥ cūrṇyante abhihatāḥ daityāḥ kācāḥ ṭaṅka hatāḥ iva candra udayāt samudbhūtaḥ paurṇamāsaḥ iva arṇavaḥ jayati indraḥ ca rudraḥ ca iti eva ca gaṇeśvarāḥ vāritāḥ dāritāḥ bāṇaiḥ yodhāḥ tasmin bala arṇave karaiḥ chinnaiḥ śirobhiḥ ca dhvajaiḥ chattraiḥ ca pāṇḍuraiḥ yuddha bhūmiḥ bhayavatī māṃsa śoṇita pūritā dṛḍha āhatāḥ patan pūrvam dānavāḥ pramathāḥ tathā siddhāḥ ca apsarasaḥ ca eva cāraṇāḥ ca nabhaḥ gatāḥ nadantaḥ megha śabdena śarabhāḥ iva roṣitāḥ tāraka ākhya pure tasmin surāḥ śūrāḥ samantataḥ sa śastrāḥ nipatanti sma sa pakṣāḥ iva bhūdharāḥ yodhayanti tri bhāgeṇa tripure tu gaṇeśvarāḥ ādāya parigham ghoram tāḍayāmāsa nandinam sa nandī dānava indreṇa parigheṇa dṛḍha āhataḥ bhramate madhunā vyaktaḥ purā nārāyaṇaḥ yathā nandīśvare gate tatra gaṇapāḥ khyāta vikramāḥ ghaṇṭākarṇaḥ śaṅkukarṇaḥ mahākālaḥ ca pārṣadāḥ tatas ca sāyakaiḥ sarvān gaṇapān gaṇapa ākṛtīn bhūyas bhūyas sa vivyādha gaṇeśvara mahattamān saṃjñām prāpya tatas saḥ api vidyunmālinam ādravat papāta vakṣasi tadā vajram daityasya bhīṣaṇam papāta vajra abhihataḥ śakreṇa adriḥ iva āhataḥ cukruśuḥ dānavāḥ prekṣya dudruvuḥ ca gaṇādhipāḥ duḥkha āmarṣita roṣāḥ te vidyunmālini pātite druma śaila mahā vṛṣṭim payodāḥ sasṛjuḥ yathā te pīḍyamānāḥ gurubhiḥ giribhiḥ ca gaṇeśvarāḥ tatas asura varaḥ śrīmān tāraka ākhyaḥ pratāpavān girīṇām vai tulya rūpa dharaḥ babhau bhinna uttamāṅgāḥ gaṇapāḥ bhinna pāda aṅkita ānanāḥ virejuḥ bhujagāḥ mantraiḥ vāryamāṇāḥ yathā tathā mayena māyā vīryeṇa vadhyamānāḥ gaṇeśvarāḥ bhramanti bahu śabdālāḥ pañjare śakunāḥ iva tayā asura varaḥ śrīmān tāraka ākhyaḥ pratāpavān dadāha ca balam sarvam śuṣka indhanam iva analaḥ tāraka ākhyena vāryante śara varṣaiḥ tadā gaṇāḥ mayena māyā nihatāḥ tāraka ākhyena ca iṣubhiḥ gaṇeśāḥ vidhurāḥ jātāḥ jīrṇa mūlāḥ yathā drumāḥ bhūyas saṃpatate ca agniḥ grahān grāhān bhujaṃgamān giri indrān ca harīn vyāghrān vṛkṣān sṛmara varṇakān śarabhān aṣṭapādān ca apaḥ pavanam eva ca te tāraka ākhyena mayena māyayā saṃmuhyamānāḥ vivaśāḥ gaṇeśvarāḥ na aśaknuvan te manasā api ceṣṭitum yathā indriyārthāḥ muninā abhisaṃyatāḥ mahā jala agni ādi sa kuñjara uragaiḥ hari indra vyāghra ṛkṣa tarakṣu rākṣasaiḥ vibādhyamānāḥ tamasā vimohitāḥ samudra madhyeṣu iva gādha kāṅkṣiṇaḥ saṃmardyamāneṣu gaṇeśvareṣu saṃnardamāneṣu suretareṣu tatas surāṇām pravarā abhirakṣitum ripoḥ balam saṃviviśuḥ saha āyudhāḥ yamaḥ gadā astraḥ varuṇaḥ ca bhāskaraḥ tathā kumāraḥ amara koṭi saṃyutaḥ svayam ca śakraḥ sita nāga vāhanaḥ kulīśa pāṇiḥ sura loka puṃgavaḥ sa ca uḍunāthaḥ sa sutaḥ divākaraḥ sa sa antakaḥ tryakṣa patiḥ mahā dyutiḥ ete ripūṇām prabala abhirakṣitam tadā balam saṃviviśuḥ mada uddhatāḥ yathā vanam darpita kuñjara adhipāḥ yathā nabhaḥ sa ambudharam divākaraḥ kṛta prahāra ātura dīna dānavam tatas tu abhajyanta balam hi pārṣadāḥ svar jyotiṣām jyotiḥ iva ūṣmavān hariḥ yathā tamaḥ ghorataram narāṇām yathā sadā eva niśākaraḥ saṃcita śārvaram tamaḥ diś lokapālaiḥ gaṇanāyakaiḥ ca kṛtaḥ mahān siṃha ravaḥ muhūrtam saṃkhye vibhagnāḥ vikarāḥ vipādāḥ chinna uttamāṅgāḥ śara pūrita aṅgāḥ devetarāḥ deva varaiḥ vibhinnāḥ sīdanti paṅkeṣu yathā gaja indrāḥ vajreṇa bhīmena ca vajrapāṇiḥ śaktyā ca śaktyā ca mayūraketuḥ daṇḍena ca ugreṇa ca dharmarājaḥ pāśena ca ugreṇa ca vāri goptā śūlena kālena ca yakṣa rājaḥ vīryeṇa tejasvi tayā sukeśaḥ gaṇeśvarāḥ te sura saṃnikāśāḥ pūrṇa āhutī sikta śikhi prakāśāḥ śareṇa bhittvā sa hi tārakāsutam sa tāraka ākhya asuram ābabhāṣe kṛtvā prahāram praviśāmi vīram puram hi daitya indra balena yuktaḥ viśrāmam ūrjaskaram api avāpya punar kariṣyāmi raṇam prapannaiḥ vayam hi śastra kṣata vikṣata aṅgāḥ viśīrṇa śastra dhvaja varma vāhāḥ jaya eṣiṇaḥ te jaya kāśinaḥ ca gaṇeśvarāḥ loka vara adhipāḥ ca mayasya śrutvā divi tāraka ākhyaḥ vacaḥ abhikāṅkṣan kṣataja upama akṣaḥ viveśa tūrṇam tripuram diteḥ sutaiḥ sutaiḥ adityāḥ yudhi vṛddha harṣaiḥ tatas sa śaṅkha ānaka bheri bhīmam sa siṃhanādam hara sainyam ābabhau maya anugam ghora gabhīra gahvaram yathā siṃha nāditam Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca viveśa tūrṇam tripuram abhram nīlam iva ambaram dadhyau loka kṣaye prāpte kālam kālaḥ iva aparaḥ sa ca api nidhanam prāptaḥ vidyunmālī mahā yaśāḥ tasya api eṣaḥ anayaḥ prāptaḥ na durgam kāraṇam kvacid kāle kruddhe katham kālāt trāṇam naḥ adya bhaviṣyati laṅghane kaḥ samarthaḥ syāt ṛte devam maheśvaram svāmī ca eṣām tu devānām durjayaḥ sa maheśvaraḥ tat adya darśayiṣyāmi yāvat vīrāḥ samantataḥ vāpīm amṛta toyena pūrṇām srakṣye vara oṣadhīḥ jīviṣyanti tadā daityāḥ saṃjīvana vara auṣadhaiḥ iti saṃcintya balavān mayaḥ māyāvinām varaḥ māyayā sasṛje vāpīm rambhām iva pitāmahaḥ dvi yojana āyatām dīrghām pūrṇa yojana vistṛtām āroha saṃkramavatīm citra rūpām kathām iva indoḥ kiraṇa kalpena mṛṣṭena amṛta gandhinā pūrṇām parama toyena guṇa pūrṇām iva aṅganām utpalaiḥ kumudaiḥ padmaiḥ vṛtām kādambakaiḥ tathā candra bhāskara varṇa ābhaiḥ bhīmaiḥ āvaraṇaiḥ vṛtām kāma eṣibhiḥ iva ākīrṇām jīvānām araṇīm iva tām vāpīm sṛjya sa mayaḥ gaṅgām iva maheśvaraḥ tasyām prakṣālayāmāsa vidyunmālinam āditas sa vāpyām majjitaḥ daityaḥ deva śatruḥ mahā balaḥ mayasya ca añjalim kṛtvā tāraka ākhyaḥ abhivāditaḥ vidyunmālī iti vacanam mayam utthāya ca abravīt yudhyāmaḥ arīn viniṣpīḍya dayā deheṣu kā hi naḥ taiḥ vā vinihatāḥ yuddhe bhaviṣyāmaḥ yama aśanāḥ mahā amṛta mayī vāpī hi eṣā māyābhiḥ īśvara sṛṣṭā dānava daityānām hatānām jīva vardhinī diṣṭyā tvām daitya paśyāmi yama lokāt iha āgatam dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā ca tām vāpīm māyayā maya nirmitām hṛṣṭa ānana akṣāḥ daitya indrāḥ idam vacanam abruvan dānavāḥ yudhyata idānīm pramathaiḥ saha nirbhayāḥ mayena nirmitā vāpī hatān saṃjīvayiṣyati tatas kṣubdha ambudhi nibhā bherī sā tu bhayaṃkarī vādyamānā nanāda uccais rauravī sā punar punar śrutvā bherī ravam ghoram megha ārambhita saṃnibham nyapatan asurāḥ tūrṇam tripurāt yuddha lālasāḥ loha rājata sauvarṇaiḥ kaṭakaiḥ maṇi rājitaiḥ āmuktaiḥ kuṇḍalaiḥ hāraiḥ mukuṭaiḥ api ca utkaṭaiḥ dhūmāyitāḥ hi aviramāḥ jvalantaḥ iva pāvakāḥ āyudhāni samādāya kāśinaḥ dṛḍha vikramāḥ nṛtyamānāḥ iva naṭāḥ garjantaḥ iva toyadāḥ kara ucchrayāḥ iva gajāḥ siṃhāḥ iva ca nirbhayāḥ hradāḥ iva ca gambhīrāḥ sūryāḥ iva pratāpitāḥ pramathāḥ api sa utsāhāḥ garuḍa utpāta pātinaḥ yuyutsavaḥ abhidhāvanti dānavān dānava arayaḥ nandīśvareṇa pramathāḥ tāraka ākhyena dānavāḥ cakruḥ saṃhatya saṃgrāmam codyamānāḥ balena ca te asibhiḥ candra saṃkāśaiḥ śūlaiḥ ca anala piṅgalaiḥ rūpāṇi āsan mahā ulkānām patantīnām iva ambarāt śaktibhiḥ bhinna hṛdayāḥ nirdayāḥ iva pātitāḥ nirayeṣu iva nirmagnāḥ kūjante pramatha asurāḥ hema kuṇḍala yuktāni kirīṭa utkaṭavanti ca śirāṃsi urvyām patanti sma giri kūṭāḥ iva atyaye paraśvadhaiḥ paṭṭiśaiḥ ca khaḍgaiḥ ca parighaiḥ tathā chinnāḥ kari vara ākārāḥ nipetuḥ te dharā tale garjanti sahasā hṛṣṭāḥ pramathāḥ bhīma garjanāḥ iti ca uccārayan vācam vāraṇāḥ raṇa dhūrgatāḥ parighaiḥ āhatāḥ kecid dānavaiḥ śaṃkara anugāḥ vamante rudhiram vaktraiḥ svarṇa dhātum iva acalāḥ pramathaiḥ api nārācaiḥ asurāḥ sura śatravaḥ drumaiḥ ca giri śṛṅgaiḥ ca gāḍham eva āhave hatāḥ sūditān atha tān daityān anye dānava puṃgavāḥ utkṣipya cikṣipuḥ vāpyām maya dānava coditāḥ te ca api bhāsvaraiḥ dehaiḥ svarga loke iva amarāḥ uttasthuḥ vāpīm āsādya sat rūpa ābharaṇa ambarāḥ atha eke dānavāḥ prāpya vāpī prakṣepaṇāt asūn āsphoṭya siṃhanādam ca kṛtvā adhāvan tathā asurāḥ dānavāḥ pramathān etān prasarpata kim āsatha evam śrutvā śaṅkukarṇaḥ vacaḥ agra graha saṃnibhaḥ sūditāḥ sūditāḥ deva pramathaiḥ asurāḥ hi amī uttiṣṭhanti punar bhīmāḥ sasyāḥ iva jala ukṣitāḥ asmin kila pure vāpī pūrṇa amṛta rasa ambhasā nihatāḥ nihatāḥ yatra kṣiptāḥ jīvanti dānavāḥ abhavan dānava bale utpātāḥ vai su dāruṇāḥ tāraka ākhyaḥ su bhīma akṣaḥ dārita āsyaḥ hariḥ yathā dānavāḥ niḥsṛtāḥ dṛṣṭvā devadeva rathe suram bhūkampaḥ ca abhavat tatra śatāṅgaḥ bhū gataḥ abhavat dṛṣṭvā kṣobham agāt rudraḥ svayambhūḥ ca pitāmahaḥ tābhyām deva variṣṭhābhyām anvitaḥ sa ratha uttamaḥ anāyatanam āsādya sīdate guṇavān iva ujjahāra mahā prāṇaḥ ratham trailokya rūpiṇam vṛṣa rūpam mahat kṛtvā ratham jagrāha durdharam sa viṣāṇābhyām trailokyam ratham eva mahā rathaḥ sa tāraka ākhya abhihataḥ pratodam nyasya kūbare tāraka ākhyasya pūjā artham kṛtaḥ jaladhara upamaḥ ratha caraṇa karaḥ atha mahā mṛdhe vṛṣabha vapuḥ vṛṣabha indra pūjitaḥ sa jala jalada rājitām samastām kumuda vara utpala phulla paṅkaja āḍhyām vāpīm pītvā asura indrāṇām pīta vāsāḥ janārdanaḥ nardamānaḥ mahā bāhuḥ praviveśa śaram tatas tatas asurāḥ bhīma gaṇeśvaraiḥ hatāḥ prahāra saṃvardhita śoṇita āpagāḥ parāṅmukhāḥ bhīma mukhaiḥ kṛtāḥ raṇe yathā naya abhyudyata tatparaiḥ naraiḥ puram parāvṛtya nu te śara arditāḥ yathā śarīram pavana udaye gatāḥ gaṇeśvara abhyudyata darpa kāśinaḥ mahā indra nandīśvara ṣaṇmukhāḥ yudhi Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca śīrṇa daṃṣṭrāḥ yathā nāgāḥ bhagna śṛṅgāḥ yathā vṛṣāḥ yathā vipakṣāḥ śakunāḥ nadyaḥ kṣīṇa udakāḥ yathā mṛta prāyāḥ tathā daityāḥ daivataiḥ vikṛta ānanāḥ atha tān mlāna manasaḥ tadā tāmarasa ānanaḥ uvāca daityaḥ daityānām parama adhipatiḥ mayaḥ kṛtvā yuddhāni ghorāṇi pramathaiḥ saha sa amaraiḥ yūyam yat prathamam daityāḥ paścāt ca bala pīḍitāḥ praviṣṭāḥ nagaram trāsāt pramathaiḥ bhṛśa marditāḥ yatra nāma mahābhāgāḥ praviśanti gireḥ vanam babhūvuḥ niṣprabhāḥ daityāḥ grahāḥ indu udaye yathā vāpī pālāḥ tatas abhyetya nabhaḥ kālaḥ iva ambudāḥ mayam āhuḥ yama prakhyam sāñjali pragrahāḥ sthitāḥ yā sā amṛta rasā gūḍhā vāpī vai nirmitā tvayā samākula utpala vanā samīna ākula paṅkajā pītā sā vṛṣa rūpeṇa kenacid daitya nāyaka vāpī sā sāmpratam dṛṣṭā mṛta saṃjñā iva aṅganā vāpī pāla vacaḥ śrutvā mayaḥ asau dānava prabhuḥ mayā māyā bala kṛtā vāpī pītā tu iyam yadi vinaṣṭāḥ sma na saṃdehaḥ tripuram dānavāḥ gatam nihatān nihatān daityān ājīvayati daivataiḥ pītā vā yadi vā vāpī pītā vai pīta vāsasā kaḥ anyaḥ mad māyayā guptām vāpīm amṛta toyinīm pāsyate viṣṇum ajitam varjayitvā gadādharam yatra mad vara kauśalyam vijñātam na vṛtam budhaiḥ nava ambhaḥ pūritam kṛtvā bādhante asmān marut gaṇāḥ te yūyam yadi manyadhvam sāgara uparidhiṣṭhitāḥ pramathānām mahā vegam sahāmaḥ śvasana upamam eteṣām ca samārambhāḥ tasmin sāgara samplave nirutsāhāḥ bhaviṣyanti etad ratha patha āvṛtāḥ yudhyatām nighnatām śatrūn bhītānām ca draviṣyatām sāgaraḥ ambara saṃkāśaḥ śaraṇam naḥ bhaviṣyati tripureṇa yayau tūrṇam sāgaram sindhu bāndhavam avatasthuḥ purāṇi eva gopura ābharaṇāni ca pitāmaham uvāca idam veda vāda viśāradam pitāmaha dṛḍham bhītāḥ bhagavan dānavāḥ hi naḥ vipulam sāgaram te tu dānavāḥ samupāśritāḥ yatas eva hi te yātāḥ tripureṇa tu dānavāḥ tatas eva ratham tūrṇam prāpayasva pitāmaha siṃha nādam tatas kṛtvā devāḥ deva ratham ca tam parivārya yayuḥ hṛṣṭāḥ sa āyudhāḥ paścima udadhim nardayantaḥ yayuḥ tūrṇam sāgaram dānava ālayam atha cāru patāka bhūṣitam paṭaha āḍambara śaṅkha nāditam tridaśa gaṇa patiḥ hi uvāca śakram tripura gatam sahasā nirīkṣya śatrum tridaśa gaṇa pate niśāmaya etat tripura niketanam dānavāḥ praviṣṭāḥ iti parigaṇayantaḥ diteḥ sutāḥ hi avatasthuḥ lavaṇārṇava upariṣṭāt Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca maghavā tu nihantum tān asurān amara īśvaraḥ lokapālāḥ yayuḥ sarve gaṇa pālāḥ ca sarvaśas īśvarāḥ moditāḥ sarve utpetuḥ ca ambare tadā śaṅkha āḍambara nirghoṣaiḥ paṇavān paṭahān api nādayantaḥ puras devāḥ dṛṣṭāḥ tripura vāsibhiḥ haraḥ prāptaḥ iti iva uktvā balinaḥ te mahā asurāḥ ājagmuḥ paramam kṣobham atyayeṣu iva sāgarāḥ sura tūrya ravam śrutvā dānavāḥ bhīma darśanāḥ nineduḥ vādayantaḥ ca nānā vādyāni anekaśas bhūya udīrita vīryāḥ te paraspara kṛta āgasaḥ ākrośe api sama prakhye teṣām deha nikṛntanam pravṛddha ūrmi taraṃga oghāḥ kṣubhyantaḥ iva sāgarāḥ yuyudhuḥ niścalāḥ bhūtvā vajrāḥ iva mahā acalaiḥ kārmukāṇām vikṛṣṭānām babhūvuḥ dāruṇāḥ ravāḥ kāla anugānām meghānām yathā viyati vāyunā āhuḥ ca yuddhe mā bhaiṣīḥ kva yāsyasi mṛtaḥ hi asi gṛhāṇa chinddhi bhinddhi iti khāda māraya dāraya kecid mudgara cūrṇāḥ ca kecid bāhubhiḥ āhatāḥ paṭṭiśaiḥ sūditāḥ kecid kecid śūla vidāritāḥ dānavāḥ śara puṣpa ābhāḥ sa vanāḥ iva parvatāḥ vyasubhiḥ su nibaddha aṅgaiḥ patamānaiḥ suretaraiḥ saṃbabhūva arṇave śabdaḥ sajala ambuda nisvanaḥ tena śabdena makarāḥ nakrāḥ timi timiṅgilāḥ paraspareṇa kalaham kurvāṇāḥ bhīma mūrtayaḥ sa rathān sa āyudhān sa aśvān sa vastra ābharaṇa āvṛtān mṛdham yathā asurāṇām ca pramathānām pravartate kṛtaḥ muhūrtena samudra deśaḥ sa rakta toyaḥ samudīrṇa toyaḥ pūrvam mahā ambhodhara parvata ābham dvāram mahāntam tripurasya śakraḥ nipīḍya tasthau mahatā balena yuktaḥ amarāṇām mahatā balena skandaḥ pura dvāram atha āruroha vṛddhaḥ asta śṛṅgam prapatan iva arkaḥ yamaḥ ca vittādhipatiḥ ca devaḥ daṇḍa anvitaḥ pāśa vara āyudhaḥ ca dakṣāri rudraḥ tapana ayuta ābhaḥ sa bhāsvatā deva rathena devaḥ tuṅgāni veśmāni sa gopurāṇi svarṇāni kailāsa śaśi prabhāṇi prahlāda rūpāḥ pramatha avaruddhāḥ jyotīṃṣi meghāḥ iva ca aśma varṣāḥ raktāni ca aśeṣa vanaiḥ yutāni sa aśoka khaṇḍāni sa kokilāni gṛhāṇi he nātha pitar suta iti bhrātā iti kānta iti priya iti ca api mahā asurāḥ sāgara tulya vegāḥ gaṇeśvarāḥ kopa vṛtāḥ pratīyuḥ śarīra sadma kṣapaṇam su ghoram yuddham pravṛttam dṛḍha vaira baddham śabdaḥ babhūva amara dānavānām yuga anta kāleṣu iva sāgarāṇām gaṇeśvarāḥ te asura puṃgavāḥ ca yudhyanti śabdam ca mahat udgirantaḥ mārgāḥ pure lohita karda mālāḥ svarṇa iṣṭakā sphāṭika bhinna citrāḥ kṛtāḥ muhūrtena sukhena gantum chinna uttamāṅga aṅghri karāḥ karālāḥ kopa āvṛta akṣaḥ sa tu tāraka ākhyaḥ saṃkhye sa vṛkṣaḥ sa giriḥ nilīnaḥ sa tatra prākāra āgatān ca bhūtān mahān adbhuta vīrya sattvaḥ nivāritaḥ rudra ratham jighṛkṣuḥ yathā arṇavaḥ sarpati ca ativelaḥ śeṣaḥ sudhanvā giriśaḥ ca devaḥ caturmukhaḥ yaḥ sa trilocanaḥ ca te tāraka ākhya abhigatāḥ gata ājau kṣobham yathā vāyu vaśāt samudrāḥ śeṣaḥ girīśaḥ sa pitāmaha īśaḥ ca utkṣubhyamāṇaḥ sa rathe ambara sthaḥ bibheda saṃdhīṣu bala abhipannaḥ kūjat ninādān ca karoti ghorān ekam tu ṛgveda turaṃgamasya pṛṣṭhe padam nyasya vṛṣasya ca ekam tasthau bhavaḥ saḥ udyata bāṇa cāpaḥ purasya tad saṅga samīkṣamāṇaḥ petuḥ stanāḥ ca dantāḥ ca pīḍitābhyām triśūlinā tatas prabhṛti ca aśvānām stanāḥ dantāḥ gavām tathā mūḍhāḥ samabhavan tena ca adṛśya tvam upāgatāḥ tāraka ākhyaḥ tu bhīma akṣaḥ raudra rakta antara īkṣaṇaḥ paraśvadhena tīkṣṇena sa nandī dānava īśvaram takṣayāmāsa vai takṣā candanam gandha daḥ yathā paraśvadha hataḥ śūraḥ śailādiḥ śarabhaḥ yathā dudrāva khaḍgam niṣkṛṣya tāraka ākhyaḥ gaṇeśvaram tatas siṃha ravaḥ ghoraḥ śaṅkha śabdaḥ ca bhairavaḥ gaṇeśvaraiḥ kṛtaḥ tatra tāraka ākhye niṣūdite pārśva sthaḥ su mahā pārśvam vidyunmālim mayaḥ abravīt bahu vadanavatām kim eṣa śabdaḥ nadatām śrūyate bhinna sāgara ābhaḥ iti maya vacana aṅkuśa arditaḥ tam taḍinmālī raviḥ iva aṃśumālī raṇa śirasi samāgataḥ surāṇām nijagāda idam ariṃdamaḥ ati harṣāt mṛditam upaniśamya tāraka ākhyam ravi dīpta anala bhīṣaṇa āyata akṣam iti suhṛdaḥ vacanam niśamya tat tvam sa mayaḥ suvarṇa mālī raṇa śirasi asita añjana acala ābhaḥ jagade vākyam idam nava indu mālim vidyunmālin na naḥ kālaḥ sādhitum hi avahelayā gaṇān jaghnuḥ tu drāghiṣṭhāḥ sahitāḥ taiḥ mahā asuraiḥ tena tena puram śūnyam pramathaiḥ prahṛtaiḥ kṛtam atha yama varuṇa mṛdaṅga ghoṣaiḥ paṇava ḍiṇḍima jyā svana praghoṣaiḥ abhiṣṭutaḥ satya rataiḥ tapodhanaiḥ yathā asta śṛṅga abhigataḥ divākaraḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca uvāca dānavān bhūyas bhūyas sa tu bhaya āvṛtān yat kartavyam mayā ca eva yuṣmābhiḥ ca mahā balaiḥ puṣyam sameṣyate kāle candraḥ candra nibha ānanāḥ yadā ekam tripuram sarvam kṣaṇam ekam bhaviṣyati kurudhvam nirbhayāḥ kāle kokilā śaṃsitena ca sa kālaḥ puṣya yogasya purasya ca mayā kṛtaḥ saḥ enam kārayet cūrṇam suraḥ yaḥ vaḥ prāṇaḥ balam yat ca yā ca vaḥ vairi tā asurāḥ maheśvara ratham hi ekam sarva prāṇena bhīṣaṇam vimukhīkuruta atyartham yathā na utsṛjate śaram tatas evam kṛte asmābhiḥ tripurasya api rakṣaṇe pratīkṣiṣyanti vivaśāḥ puṣya yogam divaukasaḥ niśamya tat mayasya evam dānavāḥ tripura ālayāḥ muhur siṃha rutam kṛtvā mayam ūcuḥ yama upamāḥ adya yāsyāmaḥ saṃgrāmam tat rudrasya jighāṃsavaḥ kathayanti diteḥ putrāḥ hṛṣṭāḥ bhinna tanūruhāḥ kalpam sthāsyati vā kha stham tripuram śāśvatam dhruvam adānavam vā bhavitā nārāyaṇa pada trayam adaivatam adaityam vā lokam drakṣyanti mānavāḥ iti saṃmantrya hṛṣṭāḥ te pura antar vibudha arayaḥ pradoṣe muditāḥ bhūtvā ceruḥ manmatha cāratām muhur mukta udayaḥ bhrāntaḥ udaya agram mahā maṇiḥ tamāṃsi utsārya bhagavān candraḥ jṛmbhati saḥ ambaram kumuda alaṃkṛte haṃsaḥ yathā sarasi vistṛte siṃhaḥ yathā ca upaviṣṭaḥ vaiḍūrya śikhare mahān viṣṇoḥ yathā ca vistīrṇe hāraḥ ca urasi saṃsthitaḥ bhrājate bhrājayan lokān sṛjan jyotsnā rasam balāt śītāṃśau udite candre jyotsnā pūrṇe pure asurāḥ dīpāḥ campaka puṣpa ābhāḥ na alpa sneha pradīpitāḥ tadā maṭheṣu te dīpāḥ sneha pūrṇāḥ pradīpitāḥ gṛhāṇi vasumanti eṣām sarva ratna mayāni ca jvalataḥ adīpayan dīpān candra udaye iva grahāḥ candra aṃśubhiḥ bhāsamānam antar dīpaiḥ su dīpitam tasmin pure vai taruṇa pradoṣe candra aṭṭahāse taruṇa pradoṣe rati arthinaḥ vai danujāḥ gṛheṣu saha aṅganābhiḥ su ciram viremuḥ tatra asureṣu āsura puṃgaveṣu sva aṅga aṅganāḥ sveda yutāḥ babhūvuḥ kala pralāpeṣu ca dānavīnām vīṇā pralāpeṣu ca mūrchiteṣu matta pralāpeṣu ca kokilānām sa cāpa bāṇaḥ madanaḥ mamantha khe rohiṇīm tām ca priyām sametya candraḥ prabhābhiḥ kurute adhirājyam viśeṣakam cārutaram karoti tena ānanam svam samalaṃkaroti dṛṣṭvā ānanam maṇḍala darpaṇa stham mahā prabhā me mukha jā iti japtvā smṛtvā varāṅgī ramaṇa īritāni tena eva bhāvena ratīm avāpa romāñcitaiḥ gātra varaiḥ yuvabhyaḥ rata anurāgāt ramaṇena ca anyāḥ svayam drutam yānti mada abhibhūtāḥ kṣapāḥ yathā ca arka dina avasāne ca atirasā anuviddhā vimārgitā anyā ca priyam prasannā kācid priyasya aticirāt prasannā āsīt pralāpeṣu ca samprasannā gośīrṣa yuktaiḥ haricandanaiḥ ca paṅka aṅkita akṣī ca vara āsurīṇām manojña rūpāḥ rucirāḥ babhūvuḥ pūrṇa amṛtasya iva suvarṇa kumbhāḥ kṣata adhara uṣṭhāḥ druta doṣa raktāḥ lalanti daityāḥ dayitāsu raktāḥ tantrī pralāpāḥ tripureṣu raktāḥ strīṇām pralāpeṣu punar viraktāḥ kvacid pravṛttam madhura abhigānam kāmasya bāṇaiḥ su kṛtam nidhānam āpānabhūmīṣu sukha prameyam geyam pravṛttam tu atha sādhayanti sa marmaraḥ nūpura mekhalānām śabdaḥ ca saṃbādhati kokilānām priya avagūḍhā dayitā upagūḍhā kācid prarūḍha aṅgaruhā api nārī su cāru bāṣpa aṅkura pallavānām nava ambu siktā iva bhūmiḥ āsīt śaśāṅka pādaiḥ upaśobhiteṣu prāsāda varyeṣu vara aṅganānām mādhurya bhūta ābharaṇāḥ mahāntaḥ svanāḥ babhūvuḥ madaneṣu tulyāḥ āroha me śroṇim imām viśālām pīna unnatām kāñcana mekhalā āḍhyām daitya aṅganāḥ yūtha gatāḥ vibhānti tārāḥ yathā candramasaḥ diva ante aṭṭāṭṭahāseṣu ca cāmareṣu preṅkhāsu ca anyāḥ mada lola bhāvāt saṃdolayante kala samprahāsāḥ provāca kāñcī guṇa sūkṣma nādā amlāna mālā anvita sundarīṇām paryāyaḥ eṣaḥ asti ca harṣitānām vācaḥ kaladhauta kalpāḥ vāpīṣu ca anye kalahaṃsa śabdāḥ kāñcī kalāpaḥ ca saha aṅgarāgaḥ preṅkhāsu tad rāga kṛtāḥ ca bhāvāḥ chindanti tāsām asura aṅganānām priya ālayān manmatha mārgaṇānām citra ambaraḥ ca uddhṛta keśapāśaḥ saṃdolyamānaḥ śuśubhe asurīṇām su cāru veśa ābharaṇaiḥ upetaḥ tārā gaṇaiḥ jyotiḥ iva āsa candraḥ saṃdolanāt ucchvasitaiḥ chinna sūtraiḥ kāñcī bhraṣṭaiḥ maṇibhiḥ viprakīrṇaiḥ śara vyayam prāpya pure asurāṇām prakṣīṇa bāṇaḥ madanaḥ cacāra iti tatra pure amara dviṣāṇām sapadi hi paścima kaumudī tadā āsīt raṇa śirasi parābhaviṣyatām vai bhava turagaiḥ kṛta saṃkṣayāḥ arīṇām candra prabhām aruṇasārathinā abhibhūya saṃtapta kāñcana rathāṅga samāna bimbaḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca nadat deva balam kṛtsnam yugānte iva sāgaraḥ te nānāvidha rūpāḥ ca pramatha ati pramāthinaḥ saṃkṣobhaḥ dānava indrāṇām samudra pratimaḥ babhau te ca asīn paṭṭiśān śaktīḥ śūla daṇḍa paraśvadhān śarāsanāni vajrāṇi gurūṇi musalāni ca pragṛhya kopa rakta akṣāḥ sa pakṣāḥ iva parvatāḥ modamānāḥ samāseduḥ deva devaiḥ surārayaḥ martavya kṛtabuddhīnām jaye ca aniścita ātmanām abalānām camūḥ hi āsīt abala avayavā iva vigarjantaḥ iva ambhodāḥ ambhoda sadṛśa tviṣaḥ prayudhya yuddha kuśalāḥ paraspara kṛta āgasaḥ vajra āhatāḥ patanti anye bāṇaiḥ anye vidāritāḥ chinna sragdāma hārāḥ ca pramṛṣṭa ambara bhūṣaṇāḥ vajra śūla ṛṣṭi pātānām paṭṭiśānām ca sarvatas giri śṛṅga upalānām ca preritānām pramanyubhiḥ sajavānām dānavānām sa dhūmānām ravi tviṣām āyudhānām mahān oghaḥ sāgara oghe patati api kṣudrāṇām gajayoḥ yuddhe yathā bhavati saṃkṣayaḥ vidyunmālī ca vegena vidyut mālī iva ambudaḥ sa tam tamori vadanam praṇadan vadatām varaḥ uvāca yudhi śailādim dānavaḥ ambudhi niḥsvanaḥ yuddha ākāṅkṣī tu balavān vidyunmālī aham āgataḥ dānavāḥ dharma kāmāṇām na eṣaḥ avasaraḥ iti uta śaktaḥ hantum kim ātmānam jāti doṣāt vibṛṃhasi yadi tāvat mayā pūrvam hataḥ asi paśu vat yathā idānīm vā katham nāma na hiṃsye kratu dūṣaṇam sāgaram tarate dorbhyām pātayet yaḥ divākaram saḥ api mām śaknuyāt na eva cakṣurbhyām samavekṣitum sūryaḥ tu ātma prabhāveṇa nadī arṇava jalam yathā hastena vṛkṣam utpāṭya cikṣepa gaja rāj iva vāyu nunnaḥ sa ca taruḥ śīrṇa puṣpaḥ mahā ravaḥ vidyunmāli śaraiḥ chinnaḥ papāta pataga īśa vat vṛkṣam ālokya tam chinnam dānavena vara iṣubhiḥ dudrāva hantum sa krūram mahiṣam gaja rāj iva vidyunmālī śara śataiḥ pūrayāmāsa nandinam śara kaṇṭakita aṅgaḥ vai śailādiḥ saḥ abhavat punar vilambita aśvaḥ viśiras bhramitaḥ ca raṇe rathaḥ antarāt nirgataḥ ca eva māyayā sa diteḥ sutaḥ ājaghāna tadā śaktyā śailādim samavasthitam tām eva tu viniṣkramya śaktim śoṇita bhūṣitām vidyunmālini nihate siddha cāraṇa kiṃnarāḥ iṣubhiḥ gāḍha viddhāḥ ca patanti pramatha arṇave nāgam tu nāgādhipateḥ śatākṣam mayaḥ vidārya iṣu vareṇa tūrṇam tatas śaraiḥ pramatha gaṇaiḥ ca dānavāḥ dṛḍha āhatāḥ ca uttama vega vikramāḥ bhṛśa anuviddhāḥ tripuram praveśitāḥ yathā śivaḥ cakradhareṇa saṃyuge tatas tu śaṅkha ānaka bheri mardalāḥ sa siṃhanādāḥ danu putra bhaṅga dāḥ tatas bāṇam tridhā devaḥ tri daivata mayam haraḥ ākāśam svarṇa saṃkāśam kṛtam sūryeṇa rañjitam dhik dhik mām iti cakranda kaṣṭam kaṣṭam iti bruvan vaidhuryam daivatam dṛṣṭvā śailādiḥ gaja vat gataḥ kim idam tu iti papraccha śūlapāṇim maheśvaram tatas śaśāṅka tilakaḥ kapardī parama ārta vat atha nandīśvaraḥ tūrṇam manaḥ māruta vat balī śare tripuram āyāti tripuram praviveśa saḥ sa mayam prekṣya gaṇapaḥ prāha kāñcana saṃnibhaḥ vināśaḥ tripurasya asya prāptaḥ maya su dāruṇaḥ tridhā iva hutāśaḥ ca somaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ tathā śara tejaḥ parītāni purāṇi dvija puṃgavāḥ duṣputra doṣāt dahyante kulāni ūrdhvam yathā tathā sa kapāṭa gavākṣāṇi balibhiḥ śobhitāni ca sa prāsādāni ramyāṇi kūṭāgāra utkaṭāni ca gṛhāṇi tasmin tripure dānavānām upadrave vātāyana gatāḥ ca anyāḥ ca ākāśasya taleṣu ca ramaṇaiḥ upagūḍhāḥ ca ramantyaḥ ramaṇaiḥ saha dahyante dānava indrāṇām agninā hi api tāḥ striyaḥ uvāca śatapattra akṣī kṛtāñjaliḥ dharma sākṣī trilokasya na mām spraṣṭum iha arhasi śāyitam ca mayā deva śivayā ca śiva prabha ekā putram upādāya bālakam dānava aṅganā hutāśana samīpa sthā iti uvāca hutāśanam bālaḥ ayam duḥkha labdhaḥ ca mayā pāvaka putrakaḥ na arhasi enam upādātum dayitam ṣaṇmukha priya kāścid priyān parityajya pīḍitāḥ dānava aṅganāḥ nipatanti arṇava jale siñjamāna vibhūṣaṇāḥ cakranduḥ tripure nāryaḥ pāvaka jvāla vepitāḥ yathā dahati śaila agniḥ sa ambujam jalaja ākaram tuṣāra rāśiḥ kamala ākarāṇām yathā dahati ambujakāni śīte tathā eva saḥ agniḥ tripura aṅganānām dadāha vaktra īkṣaṇa paṅkajāni śara agni pātāt samabhidrutānām tatra aṅganānām ati komalānām babhūva kāñcī guṇa nūpurāṇām ākranditānām ca ravaḥ atimiśraḥ dagdha ardhacandrāṇi sa vedikāni viśīrṇa harmyāṇi sa toraṇāni dagdhāni dagdhāni gṛhāṇi tatra patanti rakṣā artham iva arṇava oghe gṛha pratāpaiḥ kvathitam samantāt tadā arṇave toyam udīrṇa vegam vitrāsayāmāsa timīn sanakrān timiṅgilān tad kvathitān tathā anyān sahasra śṛṅgaiḥ bhavanaiḥ yadā āsīt sahasra śṛṅgaḥ saḥ iva acala īśaḥ nāma avaśeṣam tripuram prajajñe hutāśana āhāra bali prayuktam śaśāpa tat gṛham ca api mayasya aditinandanaḥ drakṣyanti tripuram khaṇḍam tatra idam nāśa gāḥ janāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca deva dviṣ tu mayaḥ ca atas sa tadā khinna mānasaḥ tatas cyutaḥ anya loke asmin trāṇa artham vai cakāra saḥ tatra api devatāḥ santi āptoryāmāḥ sura uttamāḥ śivaḥ sṛṣṭvā gṛham prādāt mayāya eva gṛha arthine virarāma sahasrākṣaḥ pūjayāmāsa ca īśvaram pūjyamānam ca bhūteśam sarve tuṣṭuvuḥ īśvaram sampūjyamānam tridaśaiḥ samīkṣya gaṇaiḥ gaṇeśa adhipatim tu mukhyam pitāmaham vandya tatas maheśam pragṛhya cāpam pravimṛjya bhūtān pitṝṇām vā api śrāddheṣu yaḥ imam śrāvayiṣyati idam śrutvā paṭhitvā ca yānti rudra salokatām Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca yadā candraḥ ca sūryaḥ ca nakṣatrāṇām samāgatau sinīvālī pramāṇa alpa kuhū mātra vrata udaye kuhū mātram pitṛ uddeśam jñātvā kuhūm upāsāte tam upāsya tatas somam kalā apekṣī pratīkṣate svadhā amṛtam tu somāt vai vasan teṣām ca tṛptaye ṛtuḥ agniḥ smṛtaḥ vipraiḥ ṛtum saṃvatsaram viduḥ pitaraḥ ṛtavaḥ ardha māsāḥ vijñeyāḥ ṛtu sūnavaḥ pitāmahāḥ tu ṛtavaḥ hi amāvāsyā abda sūnavaḥ gṛhasthāḥ ye tu yajvānaḥ haviḥ yajña ārtavāḥ ca ye gṛhamedhinaḥ ca yajvānaḥ hi agniṣvātta ārtavāḥ smṛtāḥ kālena adhiṣṭhitaḥ teṣu candramāḥ sravate sudhām ete smṛtāḥ deva kṛtyāḥ somapāḥ ca ūṣmapāḥ ca ye yasmāt prasūyate somaḥ māsi māsi viśeṣataḥ niḥśeṣāḥ vai kalāḥ pūrvāḥ yugapad vyāpayan purā devaiḥ pīta sudham somam purā paścāt pibet raviḥ evam āpyāyate somaḥ kṣayite ca punar punar yathā grathnanti parvāṇi āvṛttāt ikṣu veṇu vat tathā abda māsāḥ pakṣāḥ ca śuklāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ tu vai smṛtāḥ vyatīpāte sthite sūrye lekhāt ūrdhvam yugāntaram pūrṇamāsa vyatīpātau yadā paśyet parasparam yadā anyonyavatīm pāte pūrṇimām prekṣate divā uddiśya tām amāvāsyām yadā darśam samāgatau dṛṣṭa candrā tu amāvāsyā madhyāhna prabhṛti iha vai iti eṣa parvasaṃdhīnām kālaḥ vai dvi lavaḥ smṛtaḥ tasmāt pañcadaśe some kalā vai nā asti ṣoḍaśī śrāddhena vidyayā ca eva ca anna dānena saptadhā prajāvatām prasiddhā eṣā uktā śrāddha kṛtām ca vai iti eṣa soma sūryābhyām ailasya ca samāgamaḥ avāptim śraddhayā ca eva pitṝṇām ca eva tarpaṇam Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca tad pramāṇam prasaṃkhyāya vistarāt ca eva kṛtsnaśas tena api iha prasaṃkhyāya vakṣyāmi tu caturyugam nimeṣa tulya kālāni mātrā labdha ikṣarāṇi ca tretā trīṇi sahasrāṇi yuga saṃkhyā vidaḥ viduḥ dve sahasre dvāparam tu saṃdhyāṃśau tu catuḥśatam dve śate ca tathā anye ca saṃdhyā saṃdhyāṃśayoḥ smṛte niyutāni daśa dve ca pañca ca eva atra saṃkhyayā prayutam tu tathā pūrṇam dve ca anye niyute punar tretā yugasya saṃkhyā eṣā mānuṣeṇa tu saṃjñitā etat kali yugam proktam mānuṣeṇa pramāṇataḥ eṣā catur yuga avasthā mānuṣeṇa prakīrtitā caturyugasya saṃkhyātā saṃdhyā saṃdhyā aṃśakaiḥ saha tathā śata sahasrāṇi daśa ca anyāni bhāgaśas sahasrāṇi tu dvātriṃśat śatāni aṣṭa adhikāni ca aśītiḥ ca eva varṣāṇi māsāḥ ca eva adhikāḥ tu ṣaṭ kalpa pramāṇaḥ dviguṇaḥ yathā bhavati saṃkhyayā catur yuga ākhyā vyākhyātā kṛtam tretā yugam ca vai na uktam tretā yuge śeṣam tat vakṣyāmi nibodhata śrauta smārtam bruvan dharmam brahmaṇā tu pracoditāḥ abhivṛttāḥ tu te mantrāḥ darśanaiḥ tārakā ādibhiḥ saptarṣibhiḥ ca ye proktāḥ smārtam tu manuḥ abravīt saṃkalpitena manasā vācā vā hasta karmaṇā sarva sādhāraṇam hi etat āsīt tretā yuge tu vai saṃhitāḥ ca tathā mantrāḥ ārogyam dharma śīla tā svāyambhuve antare devaiḥ te yajñāḥ prāk pravartitāḥ śrutena tapasā ca eva ṛṣīn te abhibhavanti hi keśāḥ sthitāḥ lalāṭena jihvā ca parimārjanī śyāma prabhāḥ catur daṃṣṭrāḥ su vaṃśāḥ ca ūrdhvaretasaḥ pariṇāha pramāṇābhyām siṃha skandhāḥ ca medhinaḥ putra pautra samākīrṇāḥ mriyante ca krameṇa tāḥ eṣa tretā yuge bhāvaḥ tretā saṃkhyām nibodhata Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ varṇa āśrama pratiṣṭhānam kṛtvā mantraiḥ ca taiḥ punar sūtaḥ uvāca tān yajanti tadā devāḥ kalya ādiṣu bhavanti ye adhvaryu praiṣa kāle tu vyutthitāḥ ṛṣayaḥ tathā eṣa yajñaḥ mahān indraḥ svayambhu vihitaḥ purā te tu khinnāḥ vivādena śaktyā yuktāḥ mahā ṛṣayaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca yathā upanītaiḥ yaṣṭavyam iti ha uvāca pārthivaḥ tasmāt na hiṃsā yajñe syāt yat uktam ṛṣibhiḥ purā adrohaḥ ca api alobhaḥ ca damaḥ bhūta dayā śamaḥ tasmāt na āpnoti tat yajñāt tapaḥ mūlam idam smṛtam Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca yātrā vadhaḥ paraḥ daṇḍaḥ mānaḥ darpaḥ akṣamā balam tathā rajaḥ tamaḥ bhūyas pravṛtte dvāpare punar ādye kṛte na adharmaḥ asti sa tretāyām pravartitaḥ ekaḥ vedaḥ catuṣpādaḥ saṃhṛtya tu punar punar brāhmaṇam kalpasūtrāṇi bhāṣya vidyāḥ tathā eva ca doṣāṇām darśanāt ca eva jñāna utpattiḥ tu jāyate ete svabhāvāḥ puṣyasya sādhayanti ca tāḥ prajāḥ anṛta vrata lubdhāḥ ca puṣye ca eva prajāḥ sthitāḥ utsīdanti tathā ca eva vaiśyaiḥ sārdham tu kṣatriyāḥ ye ca anye deva vratinaḥ tathā ye dharma dūṣakāḥ divya vṛttāḥ ca ye kecid vṛtti artham śruti liṅginaḥ bhūtvā ca na bhavanti iha vedāḥ kali yuge akhilāḥ teṣām adharmiṇām śāstā bhṛgūṇām ca kule sthitaḥ lampakān āndhrakān ca api corajātīn tathā eva ca vidrāvya sarva bhūtāni cacāra vasudhām imām mānavasya tu vaṃśe tu nṛdevasya iha jajñivān saṃsthitā sahasā yā tu senā pramatinā saha tatas teṣu pranaṣṭeṣu saṃdhyāṃśe kūra karmasu utsādya pārthivān sarvān teṣu atīteṣu vai tadā arājake yuga aṃśe tu saṃkṣaye samupasthite vyākulāḥ tāḥ parāvṛttāḥ tyaktvā devam gṛhāṇi tu naṣṭe śrauta smṛte dharme kāma krodha vaśa anugāḥ saṃśrayanti ca deśān tān cakra vat parivartanāḥ tatas prajāḥ tu tāḥ sarvāḥ māṃsa āhārāḥ bhavanti hi mṛgān varāhān vṛṣabhān ye ca anye vana cāriṇaḥ bhakṣyān ca eva api abhakṣyān ca sarvān tān bhakṣayanti tāḥ samudram saṃśritāḥ yāḥ tu nadīḥ ca eva prajāḥ tu tāḥ te api matsyān haranti iha āhāra artham ca sarvaśas abhakṣya āhāra doṣeṇa eka varṇa gatāḥ prajāḥ tathā kali yugasya ante śūdrībhūtāḥ prajāḥ tathā atha dīrgheṇa kālena pakṣiṇaḥ paśavaḥ tathā matsyāḥ ca eva hatāḥ sarvaiḥ kṣudhā āviṣṭaiḥ ca sarvaśas niḥśeṣeṣu atha sarveṣu matsya pakṣi paśuṣu atha saṃdhyāṃśe pratipanne tu niḥśeṣāḥ tu tadā kṛtāḥ phala mūla aśanāḥ sarve aniketāḥ tathā eva ca valkalāni atha vāsāṃsi adhaḥśayyāḥ ca sarvaśas tāsām alpa avaśiṣṭānām āhārāt ṛddhiḥ iṣyate evam varṣa śatam divyam saṃdhyāṃśaḥ tasya vartate tatas varṣa śatasya ante alpa śiṣṭāḥ striyaḥ sutāḥ tatas tāḥ tu mriyante vai pūrva utpannāḥ prajāḥ tu yāḥ jāta mātreṣu apatyeṣu tatas kṛtam avartata yathā svarge śarīrāṇi narake ca eva dehinām evam kṛtasya saṃtānaḥ kaleḥ ca eva kṣayaḥ tathā kali śiṣṭeṣu teṣu evam jāyante pūrvavat prajāḥ manvantara adhikāreṣu tiṣṭhanti ṛṣayaḥ tu te evam yugāt yugānām vai saṃtānaḥ tu parasparam yuge yuge tadā kāle prajāḥ jāyanti tāḥ śṛṇu ete yuga svabhāvāḥ vaḥ parikrāntāḥ yathākramam manvantarāṇi yāni asmin kalpe vakṣyāmi tāni ca Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca saṃhṛtya a jānu bāhuḥ ca daivataiḥ abhipūjyate śata ardham aṅgulānām tu hi utsedhaḥ śākhinām paraḥ yathā krama upabhogāḥ ca devānām paśu mūrtayaḥ brāhmaṇāḥ śruti śabdāḥ ca devānām paśu mūrtayaḥ brahma kṣatra viśaḥ yuktāḥ śrauta smārtena karmaṇā śiṣeḥ dhātoḥ ca niṣṭhā antāt śiṣṭa śabdam pracakṣate tiṣṭhanti iha ca dharma artham tān śiṣṭān sampracakṣate taiḥ śiṣṭaiḥ calitaḥ dharmaḥ sthāpyate vai yuge yuge śiṣṭaiḥ ācaryate yasmāt punar ca eva manu kṣaye vartate satatam hṛṣṭaḥ kriyā śreṣṭhā dayā smṛtā aduṣṭaḥ vāc manaḥ kāyaiḥ titikṣuḥ sā kṣamā smṛtā para svānām anādānam alobhaḥ iti saṃjñitam maithunasya asamācāraḥ jalpanāt cintanāt tathā tena ārṣam buddhi pūrvam tu cetanena api adhiṣṭhitam pure śayanāt puruṣaḥ jñānāt kṣetrajñaḥ ucyate kṣetrajñena parijñātam bhogyaḥ ayam viṣayaḥ mama ṛṣiḥ hiṃsā gatau dhātuḥ vidyā satyam tapaḥ śrutam eṣa sat nilayaḥ yasmāt brāhmaṇastutataḥ tu ṛṣiḥ ṛṣiḥ tasmāt para tvena bhūtādiḥ ṛṣayaḥ tatas para tvena vai bhūta ādīn ṛṣikāḥ tatas śrutvā ṛṣam para tvena śrutāḥ tasmāt śrutarṣayaḥ iti evam ṛṣi jātiḥ tu pañcadhā nāma viśrutā kardamaḥ vālakhilyāḥ ca viśravāḥ śakti vardhanaḥ ūrṣaḥ atha jamadagniḥ ca vedaḥ sārasvataḥ tathā aṅgirāḥ ca eva tritaḥ ca bharadvājaḥ atha lakṣmaṇaḥ karṇakaḥ ca ṛṣiḥ siddhaḥ tathā pūrvātithiḥ ca yaḥ iti ete sapta vijñeyāḥ vāsiṣṭhāḥ brahma vādinaḥ viśvāmitraḥ ca gādheyaḥ devarātaḥ tathā balaḥ devaśravāḥ devarātaḥ purāṇaḥ ca dhanaṃjayaḥ bhalandakaḥ ca vāsāśvaḥ saṃkīlaḥ ca eva te trayaḥ ṛṣīkāṇām sutāḥ hi ete ṛṣi putrāḥ śruta ṛṣayaḥ Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ tvad mukha kṣīra sindhu utthā kathā iyam amṛta ātmikā karṇābhyām pibatām tṛptiḥ asmākam na prajāyate sūtaḥ uvāca pṛṣṭaḥ tu manunā devaḥ matsya rūpī janārdanaḥ katham śaravaṇe jātaḥ devaḥ ṣaḍvadanaḥ vibho surān udvāsayāmāsa purebhyaḥ sa mahā balaḥ tatas te brahmaṇaḥ abhyāśam jagmuḥ bhaya nipīḍitāḥ bhītān ca tridaśān dṛṣṭvā brahmā teṣām uvāca ha saṃtyajadhvam bhayam devāḥ śaṃkarasya ātmajaḥ śiśuḥ tatas kāle tu kasmiṃścid dṛṣṭvā vai śailajām śivaḥ vidārya jaṭharāṇi eṣām ajīrṇam nirgatam mune evam śrutvā tatas vākyam tam ūcuḥ ṛṣi sattamāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vajrāṅgaḥ nāma daitya indraḥ kasya vaṃśa udbhavaḥ purā yasya abhūt tārakaḥ putraḥ sura pramathanaḥ balī sūtaḥ uvāca dve kṛśāśvāya viduṣe prajāpati sutaḥ prabhuḥ kadrūḥ muniḥ ca lokasya mātaraḥ goṣu mātaraḥ tāsām sakāśāt lokānām jaṅgama sthāvara ātmanām janma nānā prakārāṇām tābhyaḥ anye dehinaḥ smṛtāḥ devendra upendra pūṣa ādyāḥ sarve te ditijāḥ matāḥ diteḥ sakāśāt lokāḥ tu hiraṇyakaśipa ādayaḥ dānavāḥ ca danoḥ putrāḥ gāvaḥ ca surabhī sutāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ vinatā putrāḥ garuḍa pramukhāḥ smṛtāḥ nāgāḥ kadrū sutāḥ jñeyāḥ śeṣāḥ ca anye api jantavaḥ tatas kena api kālena hiraṇyakaśipa ādayaḥ nihatāḥ viṣṇunā saṃkhye śeṣāḥ ca indreṇa dānavāḥ bhartāram kaśyapam devam putram anyam mahā balam samare śakra hantāram sa tasyāḥ adadāt prabhuḥ varṣāṇām lapsyase putram iti uktā sā tathā akarot vartantyāḥ niyame tasyāḥ sahasrākṣaḥ samāhitaḥ upāsām ācarat tasyāḥ sā ca enam anvamanyata uvāca śakram su prītā vara dā tapasi sthitā putra uttīrṇa vratām prāyas viddhi mām pākaśāsana bhaviṣyati ca te bhrātā tena sārdham imām śriyam bhuṅkṣva vatsa yathākāmam trailokyam hata kaṇṭakam iti uktvā nidrayā āviṣṭā caraṇa ākrānta mūrdhajā tat tu randhram samāsādya jaṭharam pākaśāsanaḥ cakāra saptadhā garbham kuliśena tu devarāj ekaikam tu punar khaṇḍam cakāra maghavā tatas vibudhya uvāca mā śakra ghātayethāḥ prajām mama tat śrutvā nirgataḥ śakraḥ sthitvā prāñjaliḥ agratas śakraḥ uvāca divāsvapna parā mātar pāda ākrānta śiroruhā ekonapañcāśat kṛtāḥ bhāgāḥ vajreṇa te sutāḥ dāsyāmi teṣām sthānāni divi daivata pūjite punar ca devī bhartāram uvāca asita locanā iti uktaḥ sa tathā uvāca tām patnīm ati duḥkhitām vajra āsāra mayaiḥ aṅgaiḥ achedyaiḥ āyasaiḥ dṛḍhaiḥ teṣām tvam pratikartum vai gaccha śakra vadhāya ca mahatām vaśam āyāte vairam na eva asti vairiṇi cakre vilolam niḥśeṣam tumbī ghaṭa karaṇḍakam tatas tu megha rūṣeṇa kampam tasya akarot mahān apākarṣat tatas dūram bhraman tasyāḥ mahīm imām tapaḥ bala āḍhyā sā tasya na vadhya tvam jagāma ha tatas gomāyu rūpeṇa tasya adūṣayat āśramam tatas tu megha rūpeṇa tasyāḥ kledayat āśramam vajrāṅgaḥ uvāca vajrāṅgaḥ uvāca kena te apakṛtam bhīru yama lokam yiyāsunā Purāṇa varāṅgī uvāca tyāgaḥ hi aprāpta kāmānām kāmebhyaḥ na tathā guruḥ śrutvā etat brahmaṇaḥ vākyam daityaḥ prāñjaliḥ abravīt vajrāṅgaḥ uvāca mahiṣī bhīṣitā dīnā rudatī śākhinaḥ tale sā mayā uktā tu tanvaṅgī dūyamānena cetasā kim evam vartase bhīru vada tvam kim cikīrṣasi iti uktā sā mayā deva provāca skhalita akṣaram vākyam ca uvāca tanvaṅgī bhītā sā hetu saṃhitam varāṅgī uvāca uvāca daitya rājānam prasannaḥ caturānanaḥ āgatya ānandayāmāsa mahiṣīm harṣita ānanaḥ cacāla sakalā pṛthvī samudrāḥ ca cakampire candra sūryau jahuḥ kāntim sa nīhārāḥ diśaḥ abhavan jāta mātraḥ tu daitya indraḥ tārakaḥ caṇḍa vikramaḥ sa tu prāpya mahā rājyam tārakaḥ muni sattamāḥ Purāṇa tārakaḥ uvāca śreyase kriyatām buddhiḥ sarvaiḥ kṛtyasya saṃvidhau aham ādau kariṣyāmi tatas ghoram diteḥ sutāḥ sthira upāyaḥ hi puruṣaḥ sthira śrīḥ api jāyate rakṣitum na eva śaknoti capalaḥ capalāḥ śriyaḥ tat śrutvā dānavāḥ sarve vākyam tasya asurasya tu sādhu sādhu iti avocan te tatra daityāḥ sa vismayāḥ sarva ṛtu kusuma ākīrṇam nānā oṣadhi vidīpitam nānā dhātura sasrāva citram nānā guhā gṛham aneka ākāra bahulam pṛthak pakṣi kula ākulam tatas sva dehāt utkṛtya karṣam karṣam dine dine māṃsasya agnau juhāva asau tatas nirmāṃsatām gataḥ tasmin nirmāṃsa tām yāte tapaḥ rāśi tvam āgate udvignāḥ ca surāḥ sarve tapasā tasya bhīṣitāḥ tārakasya varam dātum jagāma tridaśālayāt prāpya tam śaila rājānam sa gireḥ kandara sthitam uvāca tārakam devaḥ girā madhurayā yutaḥ putra alam tapasā te astu nā asti asādhyam tava adhunā tārakaḥ uvāca kṛta pratikṛta ākāṅkṣī jigīṣuḥ prāyaśas janaḥ vayam ca jāti dharmeṇa kṛta vairāḥ saha amaraiḥ taiḥ ca niḥśeṣitāḥ daityāḥ krūraiḥ saṃtyajya dharmi tām tam uvāca tatas daityam viriñciḥ sura nāyakaḥ uttīrṇam tapasaḥ tam tu daityam daitya īśvarāḥ tathā parivavruḥ sahasrākṣam divi deva gaṇāḥ yathā ṛtavaḥ mūrtimantaḥ ca sva kāla guṇa bṛṃhitāḥ abhavan kiṃkarāḥ tasya lokapālāḥ ca sarvaśas kāntiḥ dyutiḥ dhṛtiḥ medhā śrīḥ avekṣya ca dānavam parivavruḥ guṇa ākīrṇāḥ niśchidrāḥ sarve eva hi kālāguru vilipta aṅgam mahā mukuṭa bhūṣaṇam rucira aṅgada naddha aṅgam mahā siṃhāsane sthitam vījayanti apsaraḥ śreṣṭhāḥ bhṛśam muñcanti nā eva tāḥ candra arkau dīpa mārgeṣu vyajaneṣu ca mārutaḥ evam kāle tu vitate tāraka asuraḥ tārakaḥ uvāca rājyena kāraṇam kim me tu an ākramya triviṣṭapam a niryāpya suraiḥ vairam kā śāntiḥ hṛdaye mama bhuñjate adya api yajña aṃśān amarāḥ nāke eva hi viṣṇuḥ śriyam na jahati tiṣṭhate ca gata bhramaḥ svasthābhiḥ svarga nārībhiḥ pīḍyante amara vallabhāḥ sa utpalāḥ madirā āmodāḥ divi krīḍā ayaneṣu ca labdhvā janma na yaḥ kaścid ghaṭayet pauruṣam naraḥ janma tasya vṛthā bhūtam ajanmā tu viśiṣyate mātā pitṛbhyām na karoti kāmān bandhūn aśokān na karoti yaḥ vā kīrtim hi vā nā arjayate hima ābhām pumān sa jātaḥ api mṛtaḥ matam me tasmāt jayāya amara puṃgavānām trailokya lakṣmī haraṇāya śīghram senā nīḥ daitya rājasya tathā cakre bala anvitaḥ nānā krīḍā gṛha yutam gīta vādya manoharam daitya indrāḥ giri varṣmāṇaḥ santi caṇḍa parākramāḥ tārakasya abhavat ketuḥ raudraḥ kanaka bhūṣaṇaḥ ketunā makareṇa api senā nīḥ grasanaḥ ari hā paiśācam yasya vadanam jambhasya āsīt ayaḥ mayam dhvāṅkṣaḥ dhvaje tu śumbhasya kṛṣṇāyaḥ mayam ucchritam aneka ākāra vinyāsāḥ ca anyeṣām tu dhvajāḥ tathā śatena api ca siṃhānām rathaḥ jambhasya durjayaḥ parvata ābhaiḥ samārūḍhaḥ nimiḥ mattaiḥ mahā gajaiḥ śata hasta āyataiḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ turaṃgaiḥ hema bhūṣaṇaiḥ sita cāmara jālena śobhite dakṣiṇām diśam sita candana cāru aṅgaḥ nānā puṣpa srajā ujjvalaḥ jambhakaḥ kiṅkiṇī jāla mālam uṣṭram samāsthitaḥ kāla śukla mahā meṣam ārūḍhaḥ śumbha dānavaḥ anye api dānavāḥ vīrāḥ nānā vāhana gāminaḥ nānāvidha uttarāsaṅgāḥ nānā mālya vibhūṣaṇāḥ nānā sugandhi gandha āḍhyāḥ nānā bandi jana stutāḥ nānā vādya parispandāḥ ca agresara mahā rathāḥ nānā śaurya kathā saktāḥ tasmin sainye mahā asurāḥ pramatta caṇḍa mātaṃga turaṃga ratha saṃkulam na śānti gocare lubdhaḥ krūraḥ labdha samāśrayaḥ saṃtāpitaḥ khalaḥ yāti sādhya tām bhraṣṭa saṃśayaḥ sāma daityeṣu nā evā asti yatas te labdha saṃśrayāḥ jāti dharmeṇa vā bhedyāḥ dānam prāpta śriye ca kim durjaneṣu kṛtam sāma mahat yāti ca vandhya tām bhavāt iti vyavasyanti krūrāḥ sāma mahātmanām ṛju tām ārya buddhi tvam dayā nīti vyatikramam tasmāt durjanam ākrāntum śreyān pauruṣa saṃśrayaḥ ākrānte tu kriyā yuktā satām etat mahā vratam su janaḥ api svabhāvasya tyāgam vāñchet kadācana kartavya tām sa saṃcintya provāca amara saṃsadi bhavataḥ ca animittena bādhane dānava īśvarāḥ teṣām sāma ādi na eva asti daṇḍaḥ eva vidhīyatām ratham mātalinā kḷptam devarājasya durjayam kalpa kāla uddhata jvālā pūrita ambara locanaḥ rājabhiḥ sahitāḥ tasthuḥ gandharvāḥ hema bhūṣaṇāḥ hema pīta uttarāsaṅgāḥ citra varma ratha āyudhāḥ nāka pṛṣṭha śikhaṇḍāḥ tu vaiḍūrya makara dhvajāḥ japā rakta uttarāsaṅgāḥ rākṣasāḥ rakta mūrdhajāḥ gṛdhra dhvajāḥ mahā vīryāḥ nirmala ayaḥ vibhūṣaṇāḥ musala asi gadā hastāḥ rathe ca uṣṇīṣa daṃśitāḥ mahā megha ravāḥ nāgāḥ bhīma ulkā aśani hetayaḥ yakṣāḥ kṛṣṇa ambara bhṛtaḥ bhīma bāṇa dhanuḥ dharāḥ tāmra ulūka dhvajāḥ raudrāḥ hema ratna vibhūṣaṇāḥ dvīpi carma uttarāsaṅgam niśācara balam babhau gārdhra pattra dhvaja prāyam asthibhūṣaṇa bhūṣitam musala āyudha duṣprekṣyam nānā prāṇi mahā ravam kiṃnarāḥ śveta vasanāḥ sita pattri patākinaḥ matta ibha vāhana prāyāḥ tīkṣṇa tomara hetayaḥ muktā jāla pariṣkāraḥ haṃsaḥ rajata nirmitaḥ vṛkeṇa kāṣṭha lohena yamasya āsīt mahā dhvajaḥ dhvajam śatakratoḥ āsīt sita cāmara maṇḍitam camūḥ ca sā durjaya pattri saṃtatā vibhāti nānā āyudha yodha dustarā Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca sura asurāṇām sammardaḥ tasmin atyanta dāruṇe garjatām deva daityānām śaṅkha bherī raveṇa ca tūryāṇām ca eva nirghoṣaiḥ mātaṃgānām ca bṛṃhitaiḥ hveṣatām haya vṛndānām ratha nemi svanena ca jyā ghoṣeṇa ca śūrāṇām tumulaḥ ati mahān abhūt roṣeṇa ati parītānām tyakta jīvita cetasām mātaṃgena aparaḥ hastī turaṃgaiḥ bahubhiḥ gajaḥ tatas prāsa aśani gadā bhindipāla paraśvadhaiḥ śaktibhiḥ paṭṭiśaiḥ śūlaiḥ mudgaraiḥ kuṇapaiḥ gaḍaiḥ cakraiḥ ca śaṅkubhiḥ ca eva tomaraiḥ aṅkuśaiḥ sitaiḥ bhallaiḥ ca śatapattraiḥ ca śukatuṇḍaiḥ ca nirmalaiḥ vṛṣṭiḥ ati adbhuta ākārā gagane samadṛśyata ākāśa sarasaḥ bhraṣṭaiḥ paṅkajaiḥ iva bhūḥ stṛtā bhagna dantāḥ bhinna kumbhāḥ chinna dīrgha mahā karāḥ gajāḥ śala nibhāḥ petuḥ dharaṇyām rudhira sravāḥ bhagna iṣādaṇḍa cakra akṣāḥ rathāḥ ca śakalīkṛtāḥ petuḥ śakala tām yātāḥ turaṃgāḥ ca sahasraśas tatas tārā pṛthivī samajāyata nadyaḥ ca rudhira āvartāḥ harṣa dāḥ piśita āśinām vetāla ākrīḍam abhavat tat saṃkula raṇa ajiram Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca kṛta pratikṛta ākāṅkṣī dhanuḥ ānamya bhairavam śataiḥ pañcabhiḥ ati ugraiḥ śarāṇām yamam ardayat bāṇa vṛṣṭibhiḥ ugrābhiḥ yamaḥ grasanam ardayat kṛtānta śara vṛṣṭim tām viyati pratisarpiṇīm cicheda śara varṣeṇa grasanaḥ dānava īśvaraḥ viphalām tām samālokya yamaḥ tām śara saṃtatim cikṣepa mudgaram ghoram tarasā tasya ca antakaḥ jagrāha vāma hastena yāmyam dānava nandanaḥ tam eva mudgaram gṛhya yamasya mahiṣam ruṣā utplutya atha yamaḥ tasmāt mahiṣān niṣpatiṣyataḥ prāsena tāḍayāmāsa grasanam vadane dṛḍham vṛtaḥ yakṣa ayuta śataiḥ jambham pratyudyayau ruṣā jambhaḥ ruṣā tam āyāntam dānava anīka saṃvṛtaḥ uvāca prājñaḥ vākyam tu yathā snigdhena bhāṣitam maṇi hema pariṣkārām gurvīm ari vimardinīm tām apratarkyām samprekṣya gadām mahiṣavāhanaḥ daṇḍam mumoca kopena jvālā mālā samākulam sa gadām viyati prāpya rarāsa ambudharaḥ yathā saṃghaṭṭam abhavat tābhyām śailābhyām iva duḥsaham tābhyām niṣpeṣa nirhrāda jaḍīkṛta digantaram jagat vyākula tām yātam pralaya āgama śaṅkayā kṣaṇāt praśānta nirhrādam jvalat ulkā samācitam nihatya atha gadām daṇḍaḥ tatas grasana mūrdhani hṛtvā śriyam iva anarthaḥ durvṛttasya apatat dṛḍhaḥ sa tu tena prahāreṇa dṛṣṭvā sa timirāḥ diśaḥ tatas muhūrta mātreṇa grasanaḥ prāpya cetanām apaśyat svām tanum dhvastām vilola ābharaṇa ambarām sa ca api cintayāmāsa kṛte pratikṛti kriyām mayi āśritāni sainyāni jite mayi vināśitā asaṃbhāvitaḥ eva astu janaḥ svacchanda ceṣṭitaḥ mudgaram kāla daṇḍa ābham gṛhītvā giri saṃnibhaḥ grasanaḥ ghora saṃkalpaḥ saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭa chadaḥ vilokya mudgaram dīptam yamaḥ saṃbhrānta locanaḥ vañcayāmāsa durdharṣam mudgaram sa mahā balaḥ tasmin apasṛte dūram caṇḍānām bhīma karmaṇām yāmyānām kiṃkarāṇām tu sahasram niṣpipeṣa ha tatas tām nihatām dṛṣṭvā ghorām kiṃkara vāhinīm agamat paramam kṣobham nānā praharaṇa udyataḥ grasanaḥ tu samālokya tām kiṃkara mayīm camūm mene yama sahasrāṇi sṛṣṭāni yama māyayā nigrāhya grasanaḥ senām visṛjan astra vṛṣṭayaḥ kalpa anta ghora saṃkāśaḥ babhūva krodha mūrchitaḥ kāṃścid bibheda śūlena kāṃścid bāṇaiḥ ajihmagaiḥ kāṃścid pipeṣa gadayā kān ca mudgara vṛṣṭibhiḥ kecid prāsa prahāraiḥ ca dāruṇaiḥ tāḍitāḥ tadā tasya apare tu gātreṣu daśanaiḥ api adaṃśayan apare muṣṭibhiḥ pṛṣṭham kiṃkarāḥ praharanti ca utsṛjya gātram bhū pṛṣṭhe niṣpipeṣa sahasraśas kāṃścid utthāya muṣṭibhiḥ jaghne kiṃkara saṃśrayān tam ālokya yamaḥ śrāntam nihatām ca sva vāhinīm ājagāma samudyamya daṇḍam mahiṣavāhanaḥ jaghne rathasya mūrdhan yān vyāghrān daṇḍena kopanaḥ samutsṛjya ratham daityaḥ padātiḥ dharaṇīm gataḥ yamam bhujābhyām ādāya yodhayāmāsa dānavaḥ yamaḥ api kaṇṭhe avaṣṭabhya daityam bāhu yugena tu vegena bhrāmayāmāsa samutkṛṣya mahī talāt daitya indrasya atikāya tvāt tatas śrānta bhujaḥ yamaḥ tam ālakṣya tatas daityaḥ śrāntam antakam ojasā niṣpipeṣa mahī pṛṣṭhe bahuśas pārṣṇi pāṇibhiḥ nirjīvitam yamam dṛṣṭvā tatas saṃtyajya dānavaḥ jayam prāpya uddhatam daityaḥ nādam muktvā mahā svanaḥ hṛdi vivyādha bāṇānām sahasreṇa agni varcasām sārathim ca śatena ājau dhvajam daśabhiḥ eva ca jambhaḥ tu karma tat dṛṣṭvā dhaneśasya ati duṣkaram hṛdi dhairyam samālambya kiṃcid saṃtrasta mānasaḥ jagrāha niśitān bāṇān śatru marma vibhedinaḥ ākarṇa ākṛṣṭa cāpaḥ tu jambhaḥ krodha pariplutaḥ vivyādha dhanadam tīkṣṇaiḥ śaraiḥ vakṣasi dānavaḥ sārathim ca asya bāṇena dṛḍhena abhyahanat hṛdi vivyādha urasi vitteśam daśabhiḥ krūra karma kṛt sa kṣaṇāt dhairyam ālambya dhanuḥ ākṛṣya bhairavam kiran bāṇa sahasrāṇi niśitāni dhanādhipaḥ diśaḥ kham vidiśaḥ bhūmīḥ anīkāni asurasya ca cicheda laghu saṃdhānaḥ dhaneśasya ati pauruṣāt vyadhamat tasya sainyāni nānā sāyaka vṛṣṭibhiḥ tat dṛṣṭvā duṣkṛtam karma dhanādhyakṣasya dānavaḥ gṛhītvā mudgaram bhīmam āyasam hema bhūṣitam dhanada anucarān yakṣān niṣpipeṣa sahasraśas dṛṣṭvā tān arditān devaḥ śūlam jagrāha dāruṇam tena daitya sahasrāṇi sūdayāmāsa sa tvaraḥ padātiḥ atha vitteśaḥ gadām ādāya bhairavīm mahā āhava vimardeṣu dṛpta śatru vināśinīm adhṛṣyām sarva bhūtānām bahu varṣa gaṇa arcitām nānā candana digdha aṅgām divya puṣpa vivāsitām nirmala ayaḥ mayīm gurvīm amoghām hema bhūṣaṇām āyāntīm tām samālokya taḍit saṃghāta maṇḍitām daityaḥ gadā abhighāta artham śastra vṛṣṭim mumoca ha cakrāṇi kuṇapān prāsān bhuśuṇḍīḥ paṭṭiśān api hema keyūra naddhābhyām bāhubhyām caṇḍa vikramaḥ vyarthīkṛtya tu tān sarvān āyudhān daitya vakṣasi sa tayā abhihataḥ gāḍham papāta ratha kūbare cakre bāṇa mayam jālam dikṣu yakṣa adhipasya tu cicheda bāṇa jālam tat ardhacandraiḥ śitaiḥ tatas mumoca śara vṛṣṭim tu tasmai yakṣa adhipaḥ balī vyarthīkṛtām tu tām dṛṣṭvā śara vṛṣṭim dhanādhipaḥ śaktim jagrāha durdharṣām hema ghaṇṭā aṭṭahāsinīm bāhunā ratna keyūra kānti saṃtāna hāsinā sā kujambhasya hṛdayam dārayāmāsa dāruṇam tatas muhūrtāt asvasthaḥ dānavaḥ dāruṇa ākṛtiḥ jagrāha paṭṭiśam daityaḥ prāṃśum śita śilā mukham vākyena tīkṣṇa rūpeṇa marma antara visarpiṇā tena paṭṭiśa ghātena dhaneśaḥ parimūrchitaḥ khaḍga astraḥ nirṛtiḥ devaḥ niśācara bala anugaḥ atha dṛṣṭvā tu durdharṣam kujambhaḥ rākṣasa īśvaram sa dṛṣṭvā coditām senām bhalla nānā astra bhīṣaṇām rathāt āplutya vegena bhūṣaṇa dyuti bhāsvaraḥ khaḍgena kamalāni iva vikośena ambara tviṣā tiryak pṛṣṭham adhas ca ūrdhvam dīrgha bāhuḥ mahā asinā saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭa āṭopa bhrukuṭī vikaṭa ānanaḥ pracaṇḍa kopa rakta akṣaḥ nyakṛntat dānavān raṇe tatas niḥśeṣita prāyām vilokya svām anīkinīm jīva grāhān sa jagrāha baddhvā pāśaiḥ sahasraśas niḥśvasan dīrgham uṣṇam ca roṣāt tāmra vilocanaḥ dhyātvā astram gāruḍam divyam bāṇam saṃdhāya kārmuke mumoca dānava anīke tam bāṇam śatru dāraṇam prathamam kārmukāt tasya niśceruḥ dhūma rājayaḥ anantaram sphuliṅgānām koṭayaḥ dīpta varcasām tatas jvālā ākulam vyoma cakāra astram samantataḥ tatas krameṇa durvāram nānā rūpam tadā bhavat amūrtaḥ ca abhavat lokaḥ hi andhakāra samāvṛtaḥ atha abhimukham āyāntam daityam dṛṣṭvā dhanādhipaḥ babhūva sambhrama āviṣṭaḥ palāyana parāyaṇaḥ tatas palāyataḥ tasya mukuṭam ratna maṇḍitam papāta bhū tale dīptam ravi bimbam iva ambarāt śūrāṇām abhijātānām bhartari apasṛte raṇāt martum saṃgrāma śirasi yuktam tad bhūṣaṇa agratas yuyutsavaḥ sthitāḥ yakṣāḥ mukuṭam parivārya tam abhimāna dhanāḥ vīrāḥ dhanadasya padānugāḥ tān amarṣāt ca samprekṣya dānavaḥ caṇḍa pauruṣaḥ bhuśuṇḍīm bhairava ākārām gṛhītvā śaila gauravām rakṣiṇaḥ mukuṭasya atha niṣpipeṣa niśācarān dhanāni ratnāni ca mūrtimanti tathā nidhānāni śarīriṇaḥ ca ādāya sarvāṇi jagāma daityaḥ jambhaḥ sva sainyam danuja indra siṃhaḥ māyām amoghām āśritya tāmasīm rākṣasa īśvaraḥ tatas viphala netrāṇi dānavānām balāni tu na śekuḥ calitam tatra padāt api padam tadā tatas nānā astra varṣeṇa dānavānām mahā camūm jaghāna ghana nīhāra timira ātura vāhanām astram cakāra sāvitram ulkā saṃghāta maṇḍitam tatas astram visphuliṅga aṅkam tamaḥ kṛtsnam vyanāśayat praphulla aruṇa padma ogham śaradi iva amalam saraḥ tatas tamasi saṃśānte daitya indrāḥ prāpta cakṣuṣaḥ cakruḥ krūreṇa manasā deva anīkaiḥ saha adbhutam athā ādāya dhanuḥ ghoram iṣūn ca āśīviṣa upamān rākṣasa indraḥ tam āyāntam vilokya sa padānugaḥ tad ādānam ca saṃdhānam na mokṣaḥ ca api lakṣyate cicheda asya śara vrātān sva śaraiḥ atilāghavāt dhvajam parama tīkṣṇena citrakarmā amara dviṣaḥ kujambhaḥ karma tat dṛṣṭvā rākṣasa indrasya saṃyuge roṣa rakta īkṣaṇa yutaḥ rathāt āplutya dānavaḥ tam rakṣaḥ adhipatiḥ prāptam mudgareṇa ahanat hṛdi sa tu tena prahāreṇa kṣīṇaḥ saṃbhrānta mānasaḥ tasthau aceṣṭaḥ danujaḥ yathā dhīraḥ dharādharaḥ sa muhūrtam samāśvastaḥ dānava indraḥ ati durjayaḥ keśeṣu nirṛtim daityaḥ jānunā ākramya dhiṣṭhitam tatas khaḍgena ca śiraḥ chettum aicchat amarṣaṇaḥ tāḍayāmāsa gadayā dayām utsṛjya pāśabhṛt dadhāra rūpam meghasya vidyut mālā latā āvṛtam tad avasthā gatam dṛṣṭvā kujambham mahiṣa asuraḥ nirṛtim varuṇam ca eva tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭra utkaṭa ānanaḥ tyaktvā ratha patham bhītau mahiṣasya ati raṃhasā bhṛśam drutau javāt digbhyām ubhābhyām bhaya vihvalau jagāma nirṛtiḥ kṣipram śaraṇam pākaśāsanam cakre soma astra niḥsṛṣṭam hima saṃghāta kaṇṭakam vāyunā tena candreṇa saṃśuṣkeṇa himena ca vyathitāḥ dānavāḥ sarve śīta ucchinnāḥ vipauruṣāḥ na śekuḥ calitum padbhyām na astrāṇi ādātum eva ca gātrāṇi asura sainyānām adahyanta samantataḥ mahiṣaḥ niṣprayatnaḥ tu śītena akampita ānanaḥ kakṣau ālambya pāṇibhyām upaviṣṭaḥ hi adhomukhaḥ sarve te niṣpratīkārāḥ daityāḥ candramasā jitāḥ raṇa icchām dūratas tyaktvā tasthuḥ te jīvita arthinaḥ tatra abravīt kālanemiḥ daityān kopena dīpitaḥ bho bhoḥ śṛṅgāriṇaḥ śarāḥ sarve śastra astra pāragāḥ kalām pūrayitum yatnāt ṣoḍaśīm ati vikramāḥ kim prayātāḥ ca tiṣṭhadhvam samare amara nirjitāḥ na yuktam etat śūrāṇām viśeṣāt daitya janmanām rājā ca antaritaḥ asmākam tārakaḥ loka mārakaḥ viratānām raṇāt asmāt kruddhaḥ prāṇān hariṣyati śītena naṣṭa śrutayaḥ bhraṣṭa vāc pāṭavāḥ tathā mūkāḥ tadā abhavan daityāḥ tān dṛṣṭvā naṣṭa cetaskān daityān śītena sāditān matvā kāla kṣamam kāryam kālanemiḥ mahā asuraḥ āśritya dānavīm māyām vitatya svam mahā vapuḥ nirmame dānava indra īśaḥ śarīre bhāskara ayutam diśaḥ ca māyayā caṇḍaiḥ pūrayāmāsa pāvakaiḥ tatas jvālā ākulam sarvam trailokyam abhavat kṣaṇāt tena jvālā samūhena himāṃśuḥ agamat śamam tatas krameṇa vibhraṣṭam śīta durdinam ābabhau tat balam dānava indrāṇām māyayā kālaneminaḥ tat dṛṣṭvā dānava anīkam labdha saṃjñam divākaraḥ uvāca aruṇam udbhrāntaḥ kopāt loka eka locanaḥ divākaraḥ uvāca naya aruṇa ratham śīghram kālanemi rathaḥ yatas vimardaḥ tatra viṣamaḥ bhavitā śūra saṃkṣayaḥ jitaḥ eṣa śaśāṅkaḥ atra yad balam vayam āśritāḥ jagaddīpaḥ atha bhagavān jagrāha vitatam dhanuḥ śarau ca dvau mahābhāgaḥ divyau āśīviṣa dyutī saṃcāra astreṇa saṃdhāya bāṇam ekam sasarja saḥ saṃcāra astreṇa rūpāṇām kṣaṇāt cakre viparyayam devānām dānavam rūpam dānavānām ca daivikam kālanemiḥ ruṣā āviṣṭaḥ kṛtāntaḥ iva saṃkṣaye kāṃścid khaḍgena tīkṣṇena kāṃścid nārāca vṛṣṭibhiḥ kāṃścid gadābhiḥ ghorābhiḥ kāṃścid ghoraiḥ paraśvadhaiḥ śirāṃsi keṣāṃcid apātayat ca bhujān rathān sārathīn ca ugra vegaḥ kāṃścid pipeṣa atha rathasya vegāt kāṃścid krudhā ca uddhata muṣṭi pātaiḥ raṇe vinihatān dṛṣṭvā nemiḥ svān dānava adhipaḥ rūpam svam tu prapadyanta hi asurāḥ sura dharṣitāḥ kālanemiḥ ruṣā āviṣṭaḥ teṣām rūpam na buddhavān nemi daityaḥ tu tān dṛṣṭvā kālanemim uvāca ha bhavatā mohitena ājau nihatāḥ bhūri vikramāḥ daityānām daśa lakṣāṇi durjayānām suraiḥ iha sarva astra vāraṇam muñca brāhmam astram tvarā anvitaḥ yojayāmāsa bāṇam hi brahmāstra vihitena tu saṃcara astram ca saṃśāntam svayam āyodhane babhau mahā indra jālam āśritya cakre svām koṭiśas tanum tatāpa dānava anīkam gata majja ogha śoṇitam tatas ca avarṣat analam samantāt ati saṃhatam cakṣūṃṣi dānava indrāṇām cakāra andhāni ca prabhuḥ gajānām agalat medaḥ petuḥ ca api aravāḥ bhuvi turagāḥ niḥśvasantaḥ ca gharma ārtāḥ rathinaḥ api ca itas ca itas ca salilam prārthayantaḥ tṛṣā āturāḥ pracchāya viṭapān ca eva girīṇām gahvarāṇi ca teṣām prārthayatām śītam druma antara visarpiṇām dāva agniḥ prajvalan ca eva ghora arciḥ dagdha pādapaḥ toya arthinaḥ puras dṛṣṭvā toyam kallola mālinam puraḥsthitam api prāptum na śekuḥ avamarditāḥ a prāpya salilam bhūmau vyātta āsyāḥ gata cetasaḥ rathāḥ gajāḥ ca patitāḥ turagāḥ ca samāpitāḥ dānavānām sahasrāṇi vyadṛśyanta mṛtāni tu saṃkṣaye dānava indrāṇām tasmin mahati vartite prakopa udbhūta tāmra akṣaḥ kālanemiḥ ruṣā āturaḥ pracchādya gagana ābhogam ravi māyām vyanāśayat śītam vavarṣa salilam dānava indra balam prati daityāḥ tām vṛṣṭim āsādya samāśvastāḥ tatas kramāt bīja aṅkurāḥ iva āmlānāḥ prāpya vṛṣṭim dharā tale tatas sa megha rūpī tu kālanemiḥ mahā asuraḥ śastra vṛṣṭim vavarṣa ugrām deva anīkeṣu durjayām tayā vṛṣṭyā bādhyamānāḥ daitya indrāṇām mahā ojasām gatim kām ca na paśyantaḥ gāvaḥ śīta arditāḥ iva parasparam vyalīyanta pṛṣṭheṣu vyastra pāṇayaḥ apare kuñcitaiḥ gātraiḥ sva hasta pihita ānanāḥ itas ca itas ca saṃbhrāntāḥ babhramuḥ vai diśaḥ daśa dṛśyante patitāḥ bhūmau śastra bhinna aṅga saṃdhayaḥ viparyasta ratha āsaṅgāḥ niṣpiṣṭa dhvaja paṅktayaḥ evam ājau balī daityaḥ kālanemiḥ mahā asuraḥ jaghne muhūrta mātreṇa gandharvāṇām daśa ayutam yakṣāṇām pañca lakṣāṇi rakṣasām ayutāni ṣaḍ trīṇi lakṣāṇi jaghne sa kiṃnarāṇām tarasvinām jaghne piśāca mukhyānām sapta lakṣāṇi nirbhayaḥ itareṣām asaṃkhyātāḥ sura jāti nikāyinām tam āsādya raṇe ghoram ekaikaḥ ṣaṣṭibhiḥ śaraiḥ jaghne marmasu tīkṣṇa agraiḥ asuram bhīma darśanam tābhyām bāṇa prahāraiḥ sa kiṃcid āyasta cetanaḥ jagrāha cakram aṣṭa āram taila dhautam raṇa antakam vavarṣa bhiṣajaḥ mūrdhni saṃchādya ākāśa gocaram tat ca karma tayoḥ dṛṣṭvā vismitaḥ kopam āviśat jagrāha mudgaram bhīmam kāla daṇḍa vibhīṣaṇam tasya karma aśvinau dṛṣṭvā bhiṣajau citra yodhinau tatas vajra mayam varṣam prāvartat ati dāruṇam nārāyaṇa astram balavān mumoca raṇa mūrdhani vajra astram śamayāmāsa dānava indraḥ astra tejasā tau aśvinau raṇāt bhītau sahasrākṣa ratham prati prāpya indrasya ratham krūraḥ daitya anīka padānugaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā sarva bhūtāni vitresuḥ vihvalāni tu dṛṣṭvā daityasya tat krauryam sarva bhūtāni menire celuḥ śikhariṇaḥ mukhyāḥ petuḥ ulkāḥ nabhastalāt jagarjuḥ jaladāḥ dikṣu hi udbhūtāḥ ca mahā arṇavāḥ tām bhūta vikṛtim dṛṣṭvā bhagavān garuḍadhvajaḥ lakṣmī kara yuga ajasra lālita aṅghri saroruhaḥ vimṛśya sura saṃkṣobham vainateyam samāhvayat āhūte avasthite tasmin nāga avasthita varṣmaṇi divya nānā astra tīkṣṇa arciḥ āruhya āgāt surān svayam dānava indraiḥ nava ambhoda sacchāyaiḥ pauruṣa utkaṭaiḥ yathā hi puruṣam ghoraiḥ abhāgyaiḥ vaṃśa śālibhiḥ paritrāṇāya āśu kṛtam sukṣetre karma nirmalam sphurantam udayādri stham sūryam uṣṇa tviṣā iva prabhāvam jñātum icchantaḥ dānavāḥ tasya tejasaḥ tam ālokya asura indrāḥ tu harṣa sampūrṇa mānasāḥ ayam sa daitya cakrāṇām kṛtāntaḥ keśavaḥ ari hā enam āśritya lokeṣu yajña bhāga bhujaḥ amarāḥ iti uktvā dānavāḥ sarve parivārya samantataḥ kālanemi prabhṛtayaḥ daśa daityāḥ mahā rathāḥ ṣaṣṭyā vivyādha bāṇānām kālanemiḥ janārdanam nimiḥ śatena bāṇānām mathanaḥ aśītibhiḥ śaraiḥ śeṣāḥ daitya īśvarāḥ sarve viṣṇum ekaikaśas śaraiḥ daśabhiḥ ca eva yattāḥ te jaghnuḥ sa garuḍam raṇe ekaikam dānavam jaghne ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ ajihmagaiḥ ākarṇa kṛṣṭaiḥ bhūyas ca kālanemiḥ tribhiḥ śaraiḥ viṣṇum vivyādha hṛdaye krodhāt rakta vilocanaḥ tasya aśobhanta te bāṇāḥ hṛdaye tapta kāñcanāḥ mayūkhāni iva dīptāni kaustubhasya sphuṭa tviṣaḥ dānava indraḥ tam aprāptam viyati eva śataiḥ śaraiḥ tatas viṣṇuḥ prakupitaḥ prāsam jagrāha bhairavam tena daityasya hṛdayam tāḍayāmāsa gāḍhatas śaktim jagrāha tīkṣṇa agrām hema ghaṇṭā aṭṭahāsinīm tayā vāma bhujam viṣṇoḥ bibheda diti nandanaḥ padmarāga mayena iva keyūreṇa vibhūṣitaḥ tatas viṣṇuḥ prakupitaḥ jagrāha vipulam dhanuḥ saptadaśa ca nārācān tīkṣṇān marma vibhedinaḥ daityasya hṛdayam ṣaḍbhiḥ vivyādha ca tribhiḥ śaraiḥ caturbhiḥ sārathim ca asya dhvajam ca ekena pattriṇā dvābhyām jyā dhanuṣī ca api bhujam savyam ca pattriṇā sa viddhaḥ hṛdaye gāḍham daityaḥ hari śilīmukhaiḥ sruta rakta aruṇa prāṃśuḥ pīḍā ākulita mānasaḥ tam ākampitam ālakṣya gadām jagrāha keśavaḥ sā papāta śirasi ugrā vipulā kālaneminaḥ saṃcūrṇita uttamāṅgaḥ tu niṣpiṣṭa mukuṭaḥ asuraḥ prāpatat sve rathe bhagne visaṃjñaḥ śiṣṭa jīvitaḥ smita pūrvam uvāca idam vākyam cakra āyudhaḥ prabhuḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca tam dṛṣṭvā dānavāḥ kruddhāḥ ceruḥ svaiḥ svaiḥ balaiḥ vṛtāḥ saraghāḥ iva mākṣīka haraṇe sarvatodiśam kṛṣṇa cāmara jāla āḍhye sudhā viracita aṅkure tasya āsan dānavāḥ raudrāḥ gajasya pada rakṣiṇaḥ sapta viṃśati sāhasrāḥ kirīṭa kavaca ujjvalāḥ aśva ārūḍhaḥ ca mathanaḥ jambhakaḥ ca uṣṭra vāhanaḥ śumbhaḥ api vipulam meṣam samāruhya avrajat raṇam apare dānava indrāḥ tu yattāḥ nānā astra pāṇayaḥ ājaghnuḥ samare kruddhāḥ viṣṇum akliṣṭa kāriṇam śumbhaḥ śūlena tīkṣṇena prāsena grasanaḥ tathā cakreṇa mahiṣaḥ kruddhaḥ jambhaḥ śaktyā mahā raṇe jaghnuḥ nārāyaṇam sarve śeṣāḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ ca mārgaṇaiḥ tāni astrāṇi prayuktāni śarīram viviśuḥ hareḥ guru uktāni upadiṣṭāni sat śiṣyasya śrutau iva asambhrāntaḥ raṇe viṣṇuḥ atha jagrāha kārmukam tatas abhisaṃdhya daityān tān ākarṇa ākṛṣṭa kārmukaḥ nimim vivyādha viṃśatyā bāṇānām agni varcasām mathanam daśabhiḥ bāṇaiḥ śumbham pañcabhiḥ eva ca jambham dvādaśabhiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ sarvān ca ekaikaśas aṣṭabhiḥ śaram haste cicheda mahiṣa asuraḥ pīḍayāmāsa garuḍam jambhaḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ tu sāyakaiḥ bhujam tasya ahanat gāḍham śumbhaḥ bhūdhara saṃnibhaḥ chinne dhanuṣi govindaḥ gadām jagrāha bhīṣaṇām tām prāhiṇot sa vegena mathanāya mahā āhave tām nāśam āgatām dṛṣṭvā hīna agre prārthanām iva śaktyā ca mahiṣaḥ daityaḥ sva pakṣa jaya kāṅkṣayā nirākṛtam tam ālokya durjane praṇayam yathā jagrāha śaktim ugra agrām aṣṭa ghaṇṭā utkaṭa svanām jambhāya tām samuddiśya prāhiṇot raṇa bhīṣaṇaḥ tām ambara sthām jagrāha gajaḥ dānava nandanaḥ gṛhītām tām samālokya śikṣām iva vivekibhiḥ dṛḍham bhāra saham sāram anyat ādāya kārmukam bhūḥ diśaḥ vidiśaḥ ca eva bāṇa jāla mayāḥ babhuḥ dṛṣṭvā tad astra māhātmyam senanīḥ grasanaḥ asuraḥ brāhmam astram cakāra asau sarva astra vinivāraṇam saṃdhīyamāne tasmin tu mārutaḥ paruṣaḥ vavau cakruḥ astrāṇi divyāni nānā rūpāṇi saṃyuge nārāyaṇa astram grasanaḥ gṛhītvā cakram nimiḥ sva astra varam mumoca aiṣīkam astram ca cakāra jambhaḥ tad kāladaṇḍa astra nivāraṇāya yāvat na saṃdhyā na daśām prayānti daitya īśvarāḥ ca astra nivāraṇāya tāvat kṣaṇena eva jaghāna koṭīḥ daitya īśvarāṇām sa gajān saha aśvān anantaram śāntam abhūt tat astram daitya astra yogeṇa tu kāla daṇḍam śāntam tat ālokya hariḥ sva śastram sva vikrame manyu parīta mūrtiḥ jagrāha cakram tapana ayuta ābham ugra āram ātmānam iva dvitīyam cakram tat ākāśa gatam vilokya sarva ātmanā daitya varāḥ sva vīryaiḥ na aśaknuvan vārayitum pracaṇḍam daivam yathā karma mudhā prapannam dvidhā tu kṛtvā grasanasya kaṇṭham tat rakta dhārā aruṇa ghora nābhi jagāma bhūyas api janārdanasya pāṇim pravṛddha anala tulya dīpti Purāṇa nirmaryādam ayudhyanta hariṇā saha dānavāḥ paṭṭiśaiḥ muśalaiḥ pāśaiḥ gadābhiḥ kuṇapaiḥ api tīkṣṇa ānanaiḥ ca nārācaiḥ cakraiḥ śaktibhiḥ eva ca tān astrān dānavaiḥ muktān citra yodhī janārdanaḥ tatas kṣīṇa āyudha prāṇāḥ dānavāḥ bhrānta cetasaḥ astrāṇi ādātum abhavan asamarthāḥ yadā raṇe bahu kṛtvā vapuḥ viṣṇuḥ kiṃcid śānta bhujaḥ abhavat śrāntaḥ asi atha muhūrtam tvam raṇāt apasṛtaḥ bhava jaghāna bhindipālena śita bāṇena vakṣasi punar daśabhiḥ ākṛṣṭaiḥ tam tatāḍa stanāntare sa muhūrtam samāśvāsya jagrāha parigham tadā jaghne janārdanam ca api parigheṇa agni varcasā viṣṇuḥ tena prahāreṇa kiṃcid āghūrṇitaḥ abhavat tatas krodha vivṛtta akṣaḥ gadām jagrāha mādhavaḥ mathanam sa ratham roṣāt niṣpipeṣa atha roṣataḥ sa papāta atha daitya indraḥ kṣaya kāle acalaḥ yathā avasādam yayuḥ daityāḥ kardame kariṇaḥ yathā tatas teṣu vipanneṣu dānaveṣu atimāniṣu tīkṣṇa dhāreṇa śūlena mahiṣaḥ harim ardayan śaktyā ca garuḍam vīraḥ mahiṣaḥ abhyahanat hṛdi atha acyutaḥ api vijñāya dānavasya cikīrṣitam vadanam pūrayāmāsa divyaiḥ astraiḥ mahā balaḥ mahiṣasya atha sasṛje bāṇa ogham garuḍadhvajaḥ mahiṣam patitam dṛṣṭvā bhūmau provāca keśavaḥ yoṣit vadhyaḥ purā uktaḥ asi sākṣāt kamalayoninā uttiṣṭha jīvitam rakṣa gaccha asmāt saṃgarāt drutam nirmathya pāṇinā pāṇim dhanuḥ ādāya bhairavam sa citra yodhī dṛḍha muṣṭipātaḥ tatas tu viṣṇum garuḍam ca daityaḥ bāṇaiḥ jvalat vahni śikhā nikāśaiḥ kṣiptaiḥ asaṃkhyaiḥ parighāta hīnaiḥ viṣṇuḥ ca daitya indra śara āhataḥ api bhuśuṇḍim ādāya kṛtānta tulyām tayā bhuśuṇḍyā ca pipeṣa meṣam śumbhasya pattram dharaṇīdhara ābham tasmāt avaplutya hatāt ca meṣāt padātiḥ sa tu daitya nāthaḥ śaraiḥ tribhiḥ tasya bhujam bibheda ṣaḍbhiḥ ca śīrṣam daśabhiḥ ca ketum viṣṇuḥ vikṛṣṭaiḥ śravaṇa avasānam daityasya vivyādha vivṛtta netraḥ tatas asya kiṃcid calitasya dhairyāt uvāca śaṅkha ambuja śārṅga pāṇiḥ jambhaḥ vacaḥ viṣṇu mukhāt niśamya nimiḥ ca niṣpeṣṭum iyeṣa viṣṇum gadām atha udyamya nimiḥ pracaṇḍām jaghāna gāḍhām garuḍam śirastaḥ śumbhaḥ api viṣṇum parigheṇa mūrdhni pramṛṣṭa ratna ogha vicitra bhāsā tau dānavābhyām viṣamaiḥ prahāraiḥ nipetuḥ urvyām ghana pāvaka ābhau tat karma dṛṣṭvā ditijāḥ tu sarve jagarjuḥ uccais kṛta siṃhanādāḥ dhanūṃṣi ca āsphoṭya sura abhighātaiḥ vyadārayan bhūmim api pracaṇḍāḥ vāsāṃsi ca eva ādudhuvuḥ pare tu dadhmuḥ ca śaṅkha ānaka gomukha oghān atha saṃjñām avāpya āśu garuḍaḥ api sa keśavaḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca harim devaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ mene bhagnam durāhave daityān ca muditān dṛṣṭvā kartavyam na adhyagacchata athā ayāt nikaṭe viṣṇoḥ sureśaḥ pākaśāsanaḥ kim ebhiḥ krīḍase deva dānavaiḥ duṣṭa mānasaiḥ tasmāt na nīcam matimān durga hīnam hi saṃtyajet hiraṇyakaśipuḥ daityaḥ vīrya śālī mada uddhataḥ tvām prāpya apaśyat asuraḥ viṣamam smṛti vibhramam tathā eva adya iha magnānām bhava viṣṇo sura āśrayaḥ ṛddhyā paramayā yuktaḥ sarva bhūta āśrayaḥ ari hā athā uvāca sahasrākṣam kāla kṣamam adhokṣajaḥ daitya indrāḥ svaiḥ vadha upāyaiḥ śakyāḥ hantum hi nā anyatas kaścid strī vadhyatām prāptaḥ vadhe anyasya kumārikā jambhaḥ tu vadhyatām prāptaḥ dānavaḥ krūra vikramaḥ tat vaikuṇṭha vacaḥ śrutvā sahasrākṣaḥ amara ari hā tat ekādaśa rudrān tu cakāra agresarān hariḥ vyāla bhoga aṅga saṃnaddhāḥ balinaḥ nīla kaṃdharāḥ candra khaṇḍa nṛ muṇḍa ālī maṇḍita ūru śikhaṇḍinaḥ śūla jvālā avalipta aṅgāḥ bhuja maṇḍala bhairavāḥ piṅga uttuṅga jaṭā jūṭāḥ siṃha carma anuṣaṅgiṇaḥ kapāli īśa ādayaḥ rudrāḥ vidrāvita mahā asurāḥ aja īśaḥ śāsanaḥ śāstā śaṃbhuḥ caṇḍaḥ dhruvaḥ tathā ete ekādaśā ananta balāḥ rudrāḥ prabhāviṇaḥ pālayantaḥ balasya agre dārayantaḥ ca dānavān āpyāyayantaḥ tridaśān garjantaḥ iva ca ambudāḥ pracalat ca amare hema ghaṇṭā saṃghāta maṇḍite mahā mada jala srāve kāmarūpe śatakratuḥ tasthau himagireḥ śṛṅge bhānumān iva dīptimān tasya arakṣat padam savyam mārutaḥ amita vikramaḥ jugopa aparam agniḥ tu jvālā pūrita diṅmukhaḥ pṛṣṭha rakṣaḥ abhavat viṣṇuḥ sa sainyasya śatakratoḥ nānāvidha āyudhāḥ citrāḥ dadhānāḥ hema bhūṣaṇāḥ koṭiśas koṭiśas kṛtvā vṛndam cihna upalakṣitam viśrāmayantaḥ svām kīrtim bandi vṛnda puraḥsarāḥ ceruḥ daitya vadhe hṛṣṭāḥ saha indrāḥ sura jātayaḥ śatakratoḥ amara nikāya pālitā patākinī gaja śata vāji nāditā sita unnata dhvaja paṭa koṭi maṇḍitā babhūva sā ditisuta śoka vardhinī āyāntīm avalokya atha sura senām gaja asuraḥ gaja rūpī mahā ambhoda saṃghātaḥ bhāti bhairavaḥ paraśvadha āyudhaḥ daityaḥ mamarda ca raṇe devān cikṣepa anyān kareṇa tu mumucuḥ saṃhatāḥ sarve citra śastra astra saṃhatim pāśān paraśvadhān cakrān bhindipālān sa mudgarān kuntān prāsān asīn tīkṣṇān mudgarān ca api duḥsahān kopa āsphālita dīrgha agra kara āsphoṭena pātayan vicacāra raṇe devān duṣprekṣye gaja dānavaḥ tasmin tasmin mahā śabdaḥ hāhākāra kṛtaḥ abhavat rudrāḥ parasparam procuḥ ahaṃkāra utthita arciṣaḥ bho bho gṛhṇīta daitya indram mardata enam hata āśrayam karṣata enam śitaiḥ śūlaiḥ bhañjata enam ca marmasu kapālī vākyam ākarṇya śūlam śita śikhā mukham saṃmārjya vāma hastena saṃrambha vivṛta īkṣaṇaḥ dṛḍhena muṣṭibandhena śūlam viṣṭabhya nirmalam jaghnuḥ śūlaiḥ ca daitya indram śaila varṣmāṇam āhave sruta śoṇita randhraḥ tu śita śūla mukha arditaḥ babhau kṛṣṇa chaviḥ daityaḥ śaradi iva amalam saraḥ protphulla aruṇa nīla abja saṃghātaḥ sarvatodiśam śaṃbhum bibheda daśanaiḥ nābhi deśe gaja asuraḥ dṛṣṭvā saktam tu rudrābhyām nava rudrāḥ tatas adbhutam tatakṣuḥ vividhaiḥ śastraiḥ śarīram amara dviṣaḥ mṛtam mahiṣam āsādya vane gomāyavaḥ yathā kapālinam parityajya gataḥ ca asura puṃgavaḥ mamarda caraṇa āghātaiḥ dantaiḥ ca api kareṇa ca tadā kapālī jagrāha karam tasya amara dviṣaḥ dṛṣṭvā śrama āturam daityam kiṃcid sphurita jīvitam sravat sarva aṅga rakta ogham cakāra ambaram ātmanaḥ dṛṣṭvā vinihatam daityam dānava indrāḥ mahā balāḥ dṛṣṭvā kapālinaḥ rūpam gaja carma ambara āvṛtam kalpa anta ambudhara ābheṇa durdhareṇa api dānavaḥ yām yām nimi gajaḥ yāti diśam tām tām sa vāhanā palāyiteṣu sainyeṣu surāṇām pākaśāsanaḥ tasthau dikpālakaiḥ sārdham aṣṭabhiḥ keśavena ca śatakratuḥ tu vajreṇa nimim vakṣasi atāḍayat gadayā dantinaḥ ca asya gaṇḍa deśe ahanat dṛḍham airāvaṇam kaṭī deśe mudgareṇa abhyatāḍayat jagāma paścāt caraṇaiḥ dharaṇīm bhūdhara ākṛtiḥ lāghavāt kṣipram utthāya tatas amara mahā gajaḥ raṇāt apasasarpa āśu bhīṣitaḥ nimi hastinā tatas vāyuḥ vavau rūkṣaḥ bahu śarkara pāṃsulaḥ dhaneśaḥ api gadām gurvīm tasya dānava hastinaḥ dantaiḥ bhittvā dharām vegāt papāta acala saṃnibhaḥ sarvatas sura sainyānām gaja bṛṃhita bṛṃhitaiḥ hreṣā raveṇa ca aśvānām guṇa āsphoṭaiḥ ca dhanvinām gajam tam nihatam dṛṣṭvā nimim ca api parāṅmukham śrutvā ca siṃha nādam ca surāṇām atikopanaḥ sa surān kopa rakta akṣaḥ dhanuṣi āropya sāyakam tiṣṭhata iti abravīt tāvat sārathim ca api acodayat ghaṭṭayan sura sainyānām hṛdayam samadṛśyata śatakratuḥ adīna ātmā dṛḍham ādhatta kārmukam kṣipram saṃtyajya tat cāpam jambhaḥ dānava nandanaḥ hṛdaye ca tribhiḥ ca api dvābhyām ca skandhayoḥ dvayoḥ śakraḥ api dānava indrāya bāṇa jālam api īdṛśam aprāptān dānava indraḥ tu śarān śakra bhuja īritān daityaḥ api bāṇa jālam tat vyadhamat sāyakaiḥ śitaiḥ yathā vāyuḥ ghana āṭopam parivārya diśaḥ mukhe śakraḥ atha krodha saṃrambhāt na viśeṣayate yadā gandharvanagaraiḥ ca api nānā prākāra toraṇaiḥ muñcadbhiḥ adbhuta ākāraiḥ astra vṛṣṭim samantataḥ atha astra vṛṣṭyā daityānām hanyamānā mahā camūḥ vyākulaḥ api svayam daityaḥ sahasrākṣa astra pīḍitaḥ smaran sādhu samācāram bhīta trāṇa paraḥ abhavat eka prahāra karaṇaiḥ apradhṛṣyaiḥ samantataḥ ekaikena prahāreṇa gajān aśvān mahā rathān tatas surādhipaḥ tvāṣṭram astram ca samudīrayat tatas tvāṣṭre niśceruḥ pāvaka arciṣaḥ tatas yantra mayān divyān āyudhān duṣpradharṣiṇaḥ śaila astram mumuce jambhaḥ yantra saṃghāta tāḍanam tvāṣṭrasya nirmitāni āśu yantrāṇi tad anantaram yantrāṇi tilaśas kṛtvā śaila astram para mūrdhasu nipapāta ativegena ā dārayat pṛthivīm tatas tatas praśānte śaila astre jambhaḥ bhūdhara saṃnibhaḥ aiṣīkeṇa āgamat nāśam vajra astram śakra vallabham dahyamāneṣu anīkeṣu tejasā sura sattamaḥ āgneyam astram akarot balavān pākaśāsanaḥ vāruṇa astram mumoca atha śamanam pāvaka arciṣām patantībhiḥ jagat sarvam kṣaṇena āpūritam babhau vāyavya astra balena atha nirdhūte megha maṇḍale vāyunā ca ati ghoreṇa kampitāḥ te tu dānavāḥ māruta pratighāta artham dānavānām bhaya apahaḥ mahā aśanīm vajra mayīm mumoca āśu śatakratuḥ tayā aśanyā patitayā daityasya acala rūpiṇaḥ kandarāṇi vyaśīryanta samantāt nirjharāṇi tu tatas sā dānava indrasya śaila māyā nyavartata nivṛtta śaila māyaḥ atha dānava indraḥ mada utkaṭaḥ babhūva kuñjaraḥ bhīmaḥ mahā śaila sama ākṛtiḥ sa mamarda sura anīkam dantaiḥ ca api ahanat surān babhañja pṛṣṭhatas kāṃścid kareṇa āveṣṭya dānavaḥ tatas siṃha sahasrāṇi niśceruḥ mantra tejasā kṛṣṇa daṃṣṭra aṭṭahāsāni krakaca ābha nakhāni ca taiḥ vipāṭita gātraḥ asau gaja māyām vyapothayat tatas ca āśīviṣaḥ ghoraḥ abhavat phaṇa śata ākulaḥ viṣa niḥśvāsa nirdagdham sura sainyam mahā rathaḥ tatas astram gāruḍam cakre śakraḥ cāru bhujaḥ tadā kṛtaḥ tu khaṇḍaśas daityaḥ sā asya māyā vyanaśyata pranaṣṭāyām tu māyāyām tatas jambhaḥ mahā asuraḥ sura senā āviśat bhīmam pātāla uttāna tālukam sainyeṣu grasyamāneṣu dānavena balīyasā śakraḥ dainyam samāpannaḥ śrānta bāhuḥ sa vāhanaḥ yat āśritya ghaṭāmaḥ asya dānavasya yuyutsavaḥ tatas hariḥ uvāca idam vajrāyudham udāra dhīḥ na sāṃpratam raṇaḥ tyājyaḥ tvayā kātara bhairavaḥ vardhasva āśu mahā māyām puraṃdara ripum prati mayā eṣa lakṣitaḥ daityaḥ adhiṣṭhitaḥ prāpta pauruṣaḥ tatas śakraḥ prakupitaḥ dānavam prati deva rāj nārāyaṇa astram prayataḥ mumoca asura vakṣasi tatas nārāyaṇa astram tat papāta asura vakṣasi raṇa āgāram iva udgāram tatyāja asura nandanaḥ mumoca sura sainyānām saṃhāre kāraṇam param prāsān paraśvadhān cakrān bāṇān vajrān sa mudgarān kuṭhārān saha khaḍgaiḥ ca bhindipālān ayaḥ guḍān taiḥ astraiḥ dānavaiḥ muktaiḥ deva anīkeṣu bhīṣaṇaiḥ rathaiḥ sārathibhiḥ saha rudhira ogha hrada āvartā śava rāśi śiloccayaiḥ śṛgāla gṛdhra vāyasāḥ param pramodam ādadhuḥ kvacid vikṛṣṭa locanaḥ śavasya rauti vāyasaḥ vikṛṣṭa pīvara antrakāḥ prayānti jambukāḥ kvacid kvacid sthitaḥ ati bhīṣaṇaḥ śva cañcu carvitaḥ bakaḥ mṛtasya māṃsam āharan śva jātayaḥ ca saṃsthitāḥ kvacid vṛkaḥ gaja asṛjam papau nilīyatā antrataḥ kvacid turaṃga maṇḍalī vikṛṣyate śva jātibhiḥ kvacid piśāca jātakaiḥ prapīta śoṇita āsavaiḥ karaḥ ayam abja saṃnibhaḥ mama astu karṇapūrakaḥ sa roṣam īkṣate aparā vapām vinā priyam tadā parā priyā hi avāpa yat bhṛta uṣṇa śoṇita āsavam vikṛṣya śava carma tat prabaddha sāndra pallavam cakāra yakṣa kāminī tarum kuṭhāra pāṭitam gajasya dantam ātma jam pragṛhya kumbha saṃpuṭam vipāṭya mauktikam param priya prasādam icchate sa māṃsa śoṇita āsavam papuḥ ca yakṣa rākṣasāḥ mṛta aśva keśa vāsitam rasam pragṛhya pāṇinā priyā vimukta jīvitam samānaya asṛj āsavam na pathya tām prayāti me gatam śmaśāna gocaram narasya tat jahāti asau praśasya kiṃnara ānanam iti priyāya vallabhāḥ vadanti yakṣa yoṣitaḥ pare kapāla pāṇayaḥ piśāca yakṣa rākṣasāḥ vadanti dehi dehi me mama atibhakṣya cāriṇaḥ pare avatīrya śoṇita āpagāsu dhauta mūrtayā iti pragāḍha saṃkaṭe sura asure susaṃgare bhayam samujhya durjayāḥ bhaṭāḥ sphuṭanti māninaḥ tata śakraḥ dhaneśaḥ ca varuṇaḥ pavanaḥ analaḥ yamaḥ api nirṛtiḥ ca api divya astrāṇi mahā balāḥ ākāśe mumucuḥ sarve dānavān abhisaṃdhya te astrāṇi vyarthatām jagmuḥ devānām dānavān prati gatim na vividuḥ ca api śrāntāḥ daityasya devatāḥ daitya astra bhinna sarva aṅgāḥ hi akiṃcitkaratām gatāḥ tad avasthān hariḥ dṛṣṭvā devān śakram uvāca ha viṣṇunā coditaḥ śakraḥ sasmāra astram mahā ojasam sampūjitam nityam arāti nāśanam samāhitam bāṇam amitra ghātane vikṛṣya karṇa antam akuṇṭha dīdhitim mumoca vīkṣya ambara mārgam unmukhaḥ atha asuraḥ prekṣya mahā astram āhitam vihāya māyām avanau vyatiṣṭhata pravepamānena mukhena śuṣyatā balena gātreṇa ca saṃbhrama ākulaḥ puraṃdarasya āsana bandhu tām gataḥ nava arka bimbam vapuṣā viḍambayan kirīṭa koṭi sphuṭa kānti saṃkaṭam sugandhi nānā kusuma adhivāsitam prakīrṇa dhūma jvalana ābha mūrdhajam papāta jambhasya śiraḥ sa kuṇḍalam tān tu trastān samālokya śrutvā roṣam agāt param sa āviṣkāram an ākāram tārakaḥ bhāvam āviśat sarva āyudha pariṣkāraḥ sarva astra parirakṣitaḥ daṃśitāḥ lokapālāḥ tu tasthuḥ sa garuḍadhvajāḥ tatas ambudhayaḥ udbhūtāḥ tatas naṣṭā ravi prabhā tatas tamaḥ samudbhūtam na atas adṛśyanta tārakāḥ cara acarāṇi bhūtāni sura asura vibhedataḥ tat dvidhā api ekatām yātam dadṛśuḥ prekṣakāḥ iva tat tatra adṛśyat akhilam khilībhūta vibhūtikam astrāṇi tejāṃsi dhanāni dhairyam senā balam vīrya parākramau ca sattva ojasām tad nikaram babhūva sura asurāṇām tapasaḥ balena bāṇaiḥ anala kalpa agraiḥ bibhiduḥ tārakam hṛdi sa tān a cintya daitya indraḥ sura bāṇān gatān hṛdi navabhiḥ navabhiḥ bāṇaiḥ surān vivyādha dānavaḥ tatas acchinnam śara vrātam saṃgrāme mumucuḥ surāḥ anantaram ca kāntānām aśru pātam iva aniśam tat aprāptam viyati eva nāśayāmāsa dānavaḥ sunirmalam krama āyātam ku putraḥ svam mahā kulam bāṇaiḥ vyoma diśaḥ pṛthvīm pūrayāmāsa dānavaḥ cicheda puṅkha deśeṣu svake sthāne ca lāghavāt karṇa anta kṛṣṭaiḥ vimalaiḥ suvarṇa rajata ujjvalaiḥ śāstra arthaiḥ saṃśaya prāptān yathārthān vai vikalpitaiḥ tatas śatena bāṇānām śakram vivyādha dānavaḥ nārāyaṇam ca saptatyā navatyā ca hutāśanam daśabhiḥ mārutam mūrdhni yamam daśabhiḥ eva ca dhanadam ca eva saptatyā varuṇam ca tathā aṣṭabhiḥ viṃśatyā nirṛtim daityaḥ punar ca aṣṭābhiḥ eva ca tathā ca mātalim daityaḥ vivyādha tribhiḥ āśugaiḥ punar ca daityaḥ devānām tilaśas nata parvabhiḥ cakāra varma jātāni cicheda ca dhanūṃṣi tu tatas vikavacāḥ devāḥ vidhanuṣkāḥ śaraiḥ kṛtāḥ atha anyāni cāpāni tasmin sa roṣāḥ raṇe lokapālāḥ gṛhītvā samantāt śaraiḥ akṣayaiḥ dānava indram tatakṣuḥ tadā dānavaḥ amarṣa saṃrakta netraḥ śarān agni kalpān vavarṣa amarāṇām tatas bāṇam ādāya kalpa anala ābham śarābhyām jaghāna aṃsa mūle salīlam tatas keśavasya apatat śārṅgam agre śaraiḥ agni kalpaiḥ jaleśasya kāyam raṇe aśoṣayat durjayaḥ daitya rājaḥ śaraiḥ agni kalpaiḥ cakāra āśu daityaḥ tathā rākṣasān bhīta bhītān diśāsu pṛṣatkaiḥ ca rūkṣaiḥ vikāra prayuktam cakāra anilam līlayā eva asura īśaḥ kṣaṇāt labdha cittāḥ svayam viṣṇu śakra anala ādyāḥ su saṃhatya tīkṣṇaiḥ pṛṣatkaiḥ pracakruḥ pracaṇḍena daityena sārdham mahā saṃgaram saṃgara grāsa kalpam athā ānamya cāpam hariḥ tīkṣṇa bāṇaiḥ hanat sārathim daitya rājasya hṛdyam dhvajam dhūmaketuḥ kirīṭam mahā indraḥ dhana īśaḥ dhanuḥ kāñcana ānaddha pṛṣṭham yamaḥ bāhu daṇḍam ratha aṅgāni vāyuḥ niśā cāriṇām īśvarasya api varma daitya nāthaḥ kṛtam saṃkhye sva bāhu yuga bāndhavaḥ mumoca mudgaram bhīmam sahasrākṣāya saṃgare dṛṣṭvā mudgaram āyāntam anivāryam atha ambare rathāt āplutya dharaṇīm agamat pākaśāsanaḥ sa ratham cūrṇayāmāsa na mamāra ca mātaliḥ gṛhītvā paṭṭiśam daityaḥ jaghāna urasi keśavam yamam ca pātayāmāsa bhūmau daityaḥ bhuśuṇḍinā vāyum ca dorbhyām utkṣipya pātayāmāsa bhū tale dhaneśam ca dhanuṣkoṭyā kuṭṭayāmāsa kopanaḥ labdha saṃjñaḥ kṣaṇāt viṣṇuḥ cakram jagrāha durdharam dānava indra vasā āsiktam piśitāśanaka unmukham mumoca dānava indrasya dṛḍham vakṣasi keśavaḥ vyaśīryata tatas kāye nīlotpalam iva aśmani yasmin jaya āśā śakrasya dānava indra raṇe tu abhūt tārakasya su saṃprāpya śarīram śaurya śālinaḥ vyaśīryata vikīrṇa arciḥ śatadhā khaṇḍa tām gatam vināśam agamat muktam vāyunā asura vakṣasi jvalitam jvalana ābhāsam aṅkuśam kuliśam yathā vināśam āgatam dṛṣṭvā vāyuḥ ca aṅkuśam āhave ruṣṭaḥ śaila indram utpāṭya puṣpita druma kandaram mahīdharam tam āyāntam daityaḥ smita mukhaḥ tadā kalpa anta dahana ālokām ajayyām jvalanaḥ tatas śaktim cikṣepa durdharṣām dānava indrāya saṃyuge navā śirīṣa mālā iva sā asya vakṣasi arājata tatas khaḍgam samākṛṣya kośāt ākāśa nirmalam bhāsita asita digbhāgam lokapālaḥ api nirṛtiḥ patitaḥ ca agamat khaḍgaḥ sa śīghram śata khaṇḍa tām mumoca pāśam daityasya bhuja bandha abhilāṣukaḥ sphuṭita krakaca krūra daśana āliḥ mahā hanuḥ trailokya lakṣmīḥ tad deśe prāviśat sva puram yathā tatas kiṃnara gandharva nāga nārī vinoditaiḥ kṣaṇam vinodyamānaḥ tu pracalat maṇi kuṇḍalaḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca daitya indram arka vṛndānām bibhratam bhāsvaram vapuḥ devāḥ ūcuḥ vyaktim nītvā tvam vapuḥ svam mahimnā tasmāt aṇḍāt sābhidhānāt acintyaḥ vyaktam devāḥ janmanaḥ śāśvatasya dyauḥ te mūrdhā locane candra sūryau vyālāḥ keśāḥ śrotra randhrāḥ diśaḥ te pādau bhūmiḥ nābhi randhre samudrāḥ ye anye sūkṣmāḥ santi tebhyaḥ abhigītaḥ sthūlāḥ bhāvāḥ ca āvṛtāraḥ ca teṣām tebhyaḥ sthūlaiḥ taiḥ purāṇaiḥ pratītaḥ bhūtam bhavyam ca evam udbhūti bhājām bhāve bhāve bhāvitam tvā yunakti yuktam yuktam vyakti bhāvāt nirasya akiṃcitkaratām yātaḥ karaḥ te na vibhāsate ditijasya śarīram avāpya gatam śatadhā mati bhedam iva alpa manāḥ āsāra dhūli dhvasta aṅgāḥ dvāra sthāḥ smaḥ kadarthinaḥ mayā api upāyaḥ sa kṛtaḥ yathā evam hi bhaviṣyati jagmuḥ tam praṇipatya īśam yathāyogam divaukasaḥ samāpta niyamā devī yadā ca umā bhaviṣyati tanuḥ tava api sahajā sā ekānaṃśā bhaviṣyati tvam ca kīrtimatām kīrtiḥ tvam mūrtiḥ sarva dehinām tvam kāntiḥ kṛta bhūṣāṇām tvam śāntiḥ duḥkha karmaṇām saṃbhūtiḥ tvam padārthānām sthitiḥ tvam lokapālinī tvam kālarātriḥ niḥśeṣa bhuvana āvali nāśinī ajayat bhūṣayat ca api niḥsādhāraiḥ naga ātmajā śīghram tad udyamaḥ sarvaiḥ asmad pakṣaiḥ vidhīyatām nāradaḥ uvāca aho avatāritāḥ sarve saṃniveśe mahā gire na lakṣayāmaḥ śaila indra śiṣyate kandara udarāt himavān uvāca krameṇa āśrama saṃprāptiḥ brahmacāri vratāt anu saubhāgya dhana putra āyuḥ pati lābha anuśaṃsanam tvam me sarvam vijānāsi satya vāc asi ca api atas muhyāmi muni śārdūla hṛdayam dīryati iva me nāradaḥ uvāca harṣa sthāne api mahati tvayā duḥkham nirūpyate viṣṇuḥ yuge yuge jātaḥ nānā jātiḥ mahā tanuḥ manyase māyayā jātam viṣṇum ca api yuge yuge yathā proktam tadā pādau sva chāyā avyabhicāriṇau caraṇau padma saṃkāśau asyāḥ svaccha nakha ujjvalau sūtaḥ uvāca himavān uvāca yadi vācām adhīśaḥ syām tvad guṇānām vicāraṇe tava asmān prati cāpalyam vyaktam mama mahā mune nāradaḥ uvāca śakraḥ uvāca kāmaḥ uvāca adhyāśritam ca yat saukhyam bhavatā naṣṭa ceṣṭitam pramādāt atha vibhraśyet īśam prati vicintyatām prāk eva ca iha dṛśyante bhūtānām kārya saṃbhavāḥ śakraḥ uvāca sa bāhya antara viddhena hareṇa smara mārgaṇaḥ rāga sneha samiddha antar dhāvan tīvra hutāśanaḥ samprāpya sneha saṃpṛktam kāminām hṛdayam kila latām pavitraka sthāne pāṇau parabhṛtām sakhīm namaḥ śivāya astu nirāmayāya namaḥ śivāya astu
