anvitā vistāram tad pramāṇam ca mūle ca agre tatas ūrdhvatas mekhalā tad tri bhāgena khātam ca eva pramāṇataḥ athavā pāda hīnam tu śobhanam kārayet sadā uttara stham praṇālam ca pramāṇāt adhikārayet sthaṇḍilāyām atha ārogyam dhanam dhānyam ca puṣkalam maṇḍalāyām bhavet kīrtiḥ vara dā pūrṇa candrikā āyuḥ pradā bhavet vajrā padmā saubhāgya dā bhavet putra pradā ardhacandrā syāt trikoṇa śatru nāśinī devasya yajana arthantu pīṭikāḥ daśa kīrtitāḥ śaile śaila mayī dadyāt pārthive pārthivī tathā dāru je dāru jām kuryāt miśre miśrām tathā eva ca arccā āyāmā saman dairghyam liṅga āyāma saman tathā yasya devasya yā patnī tām pīṭe parikalpayet Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca su snigdham ca suvarṇam ca liṅgam kuryāt vicakṣaṇaḥ prāsādasya pramāṇena liṅga mānam vidhīyate liṅga mānena vā vidyāt prāsādam śubha lakṣaṇam prāguttareṇa dakṣiṇā parayā śritam pūrveṇa ca aparam dvāram māhendram dakṣiṇa uttaram dvāram vibhajya ekāviṃśati bhāgikam bhājite navadhā garbhe mādhyamam pāñcabhāgikam nābhi ardham aṣṭa bhāgena vibhajya atha samam budhaiḥ aṣṭa asram madhyamam jñeyam bhāgam liṅgasya vai dhruvam vikīrṇe ced tatas gṛhya koṇābhyām lāñchayet budhaḥ aṣṭa asram kārayet tadvat ūrdhvam api evam eva tu ṣoḍaśa astrī kṛtam paścāt vartulam kārayet tatas tasya devasya nābhyām vai kuṇḍalīkṛtam māheśvaram ūrdhva vṛttam tu avasthitam aṣṭa asraḥ vaiṣṇavaḥ bhāgaḥ madhyaḥ tasya udāhṛtaḥ evam pramāṇa saṃyuktam liṅga vṛddhi pradam bhavet tathā anyat api vakṣyāmi garbha mānam pramāṇataḥ adhastāt catur asram tu aṣṭa asram madhya bhāgataḥ śiraḥ pradakṣiṇam tasya saṃkṣiptam mūlataḥ nyaset jyeṣṭha pūjyam bhavet liṅgam adhastāt vipulam ca yat śirasā ca sadā nimnam manojñam lakṣaṇa anvitam evam ratna mayam kuryāt sphāṭikam pārthivam tathā śubham dāru mayam ca api yat vā manasi rocate Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ devatānām atha etāsām pratiṣṭā vidhim uttamam vada sūta yathānyāyam sarveṣām api aśeṣatas sūtaḥ uvāca kuṇḍa maṇḍapa vedīnām pramāṇam ca yathākramam caitre vā phālgune vā api jyeṣṭe vā mādhave tathā māghe vā sarva devānām pratiṣṭā śubha dā bhavet daśaṇī paurṇamāsī ca tathā śreṣṭā trayodaśī āsu pratiṣṭāḥ vidhivat kṛtvā bahu phalāḥ labhet āṣāḍhe dve tathā mūlam uttarā dvayam eva ca jyeṣṭā śravaṇa rohiṇyaḥ pūrvābhādrapadā tathā hasta aśvinī revatī ca puṣyaḥ mṛgaśiraḥ tathā anurādhā tathā svātī pratiṣṭā ādiṣu śasyate ebhiḥ nirīkṣitam lagnam nakṣatram ca praśasyate lagne ṛkṣe prakurvīta pratiṣṭā ādikam uttamam prājāpatye tu śayanam śvete tu utthāpanam tathā muhūrte sthāpanam kuryāt punar bāhye vicakṣaṇaḥ prāsādasya uttarā iva api pūrve vā maṇḍapaḥ bhavet hastān ṣoḍaśa kurvīta daśa dvādaśa vā punar pañca sapta api caturaḥ karān kurvīta vedikām plakṣa dvāram bhavet pūrvam yāmye ca audumbaram bhavet su upaliptam tathā ślakṣṇam bhū talam syāt su śobhanam kṛtvā evam maṇḍapam pūrvam catur dvāreṣu vinyaset avraṇān kalaśān aṣṭau jvalat kāñcana garbhitān dhvaja ādi rohaṇam kāryam maṇḍapasya samantataḥ dhvajān ca lokapālānām sarva dikṣu niveśayet patākā jalada ākārā madhye syāt maṇḍapasya tu ūrdhvam tu brahmaṇaḥ deyam tu adhastāt śeṣa vāsukeḥ saṃhitāyān tu ye tad daivatyāḥ śrutau smṛtāḥ taiḥ pūjā lokapālānām kartavyā ca samantataḥ athavā sapta rātram tu kāryam syāt adhivāsanam evam sa toraṇam kṛtvā adhivāsanam uttamam ānīya liṅgam arccām vā śilpinaḥ pūjayet budhaḥ vastra ābharaṇa ratnaiḥ ca ye api tad paricārakāḥ akṣṇoḥ uddharaṇam vakṣye liṅgasya api samāsatas viprāṇām ca arcanam kuryyāt dadyāt śaktyā ca dakṣiṇām gām mahīm kanakañca eva sthāpakāya nivedayet lakṣaṇam kārayet bhaktyā mantreṇa anena vai dvijaḥ mantraḥ ayam sarva devānām netra jyotiṣu api smṛtaḥ maṅgalyāni ca vādyāni brahmaghoṣam sa gītakam vṛddhi artham kārayet vidvān amaṅgalya vināśanam lakṣaṇa uddharaṇam vakṣye liṅgasya su samāhitaḥ tridhā vibhajya pūjyāyām lakṣaṇam syāt vibhājakam lekhā kartavyam yava aṣṭa antara saṃyutam nimnam yava pramāṇena jyeṣṭhaliṅgasya kārayet aṣṭa bhaktam tatas kṛtvā tyaktvā bhāga trayam budhaḥ bhrāmyate pañca bhāga ūrdhvam kārayet saṅgam antatas rekhayoḥ saṅgame tadvat pṛṣṭe bhāga dvayam bhavet evam etat samākhyātam samāsāt lakṣaṇam mayā Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca sthāpakasya samāsena lakṣaṇam śruṇuta dvijāḥ kṛṣṇasāra maye deśe utpannaḥ ca śubha ākṛtiḥ śauca ācāra paraḥ nityam pāṣaṇḍa kula nispṛhaḥ ūha apoha artha tatva jñaḥ vāstuśāstrasya pāragaḥ mūrtipāḥ tu dvijāḥ ca eva kulīnāḥ ṛjavaḥ tathā dvātriṃśat ṣoḍaśa atha api aṣṭau vā śrutipāragāḥ jyeṣṭha madhya kaniṣṭeṣu prakīrtitā tatas liṅgam atha arcām vā nītvā snapana maṇḍapam pañcagavya kaṣāyeṇa mṛdbhiḥ bhasma udakena vā samudrajyeṣṭha mantreṇa āpaḥ divyā iti ca aparaḥ yāsām rājā iti mantraḥ tu ca aparaḥ evam snāpya tatas devam pūjya gandha anulepanaiḥ amūru jā iti ca tathā rathe tiṣṭa iti ca aparaḥ rathe brahma rate vā api dhṛtām śilpi gaṇena tu āropya ca tatas vidvān ākṛṣṇena praveśayet tatas prāstīryya śayyāyām sthāpayet śanakais budhaḥ tatas tu nidrā kalaśam vastra kāñcana saṃyatam āpodevī iti mantreṇa āpaḥ asmāt mātaraḥ api ca tatas dukūla paṭṭaiḥ ca ācchādya netra upadhānakam dadyat śirasi devasya kauśeyam vā vicakṣaṇaḥ dukūla paṭṭaiḥ kārpāsaiḥ nānā citraiḥ vā pārśvatas sthāpayet tatra vitānam puṣpa saṃyutam kṣīram kṣaudram ghṛtam tadvat bhakṣya bhojyāni apāyasaiḥ balim dadyāt prayatnena mantreṇa anena bhūriśas caturaḥ dvārapālān ca dvāreṣu viniveśayet śrīsūktam pāvamānam ca somasūktam su maṅgalam tathā ca śāntikādhyāyam indrasūktam tathā eva ca rakṣoghnam ca tathā sūktam pūrvatas bahvṛcaḥ japet tathā puruṣasūktam ca rudrasūktam sa śāntikam tathā parājitā devī sapta sūktam sa raudrakam śāntikaiḥ pauṣṭikaiḥ tadvat mantraiḥ vyāhṛti pūrvakaiḥ palāśaḥ dumbara aśvattha apāmārgaḥ śamī tathā hutvā sahasram ekaikam devam pāde tu saṃspṛśet nābhi madhyam tathā vakṣaḥ śiraḥ ca api ālabhet punar hasta mātreṣu kuṇḍeṣu mūrtipāḥ sarvatas diśam vitasti mātrā yoniḥ syat gaja uṣṭha sadṛśī tathā āyata acchidra saṃyuktā pārśvatas kalayā ucchritā kuṇḍāt kalā anusāreṇa sarvatas catur aṅgulā evam navasu kuṇḍeṣu lakṣaṇam ca eva dṛśyate āgneya śākra yāmyeṣu hotavyam udak ānanaiḥ śāntayaḥ lokapālebhyaḥ mūrtibhyaḥ kramaśas tathā tathā mūrti adhidevānām homam kuryāt samāhitaḥ jalam vāyuḥ tathā somaḥ ākāśaḥ ca aṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ devasya mūrtayaḥ tvaṣṭā vā etāḥ kuṇḍeṣu saṃsmaret etāsām adhipān vakṣye pavitrān mūrti nāmataḥ pṛthvīm pāti sarvaḥ ca paśupaḥ ca agnim eva ca bhavaḥ jalam sadā pāti vāyum īśānaḥ eva ca mahādevaḥ tathā candra bhīmaḥ ca ākāśam eva ca tathā śānti ghaṭam kuryyāt prati kuṇḍeṣu sannyaset śata ante vā sahasra ante sampūrṇāhutiḥ iṣyate sama pādaḥ pṛthivyān tu praśānta ātmā vinikṣipet āhutīnām tu sampātam pūrṇa kumbheṣu vai nyaset mūla madhya uttamāṅgeṣu devam tena avasecayet sthitaḥ ca snāpayet tena sampāta āhuti vāriṇā punar punar prakurvīta homaḥ kāryaḥ punar punar vicitraiḥ hema kaṭakaiḥ hemasūtra aṃgulīyakaiḥ bhojanam ca api dātavyam yāvat syāt adhivāsanam brāhmaṇān bhojayet pūrvam śeṣān varṇān tu kāmataḥ rātrau mahā utsavaḥ kāryaḥ nṛtya gītaka maṅgalaiḥ sadā pūjyāḥ prayatnena caturthī karma yāvatā Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca kṛtvā adhivāsam devānām śubham kuryyāt samāhitaḥ prāsādasya anurūpeṇa mānam liṅgasya vā punar āśrayet kiñcid īśānīm madhyam jñātvā diśam budhaḥ īśānīm āśritam devam pūjayanti divaukasaḥ śubham syāt aśubham proktam anyathā sthāpanam budhaiḥ adhas kūrmaśilā proktā sadā brahma śilā adhikā upari avasthitā tasyāḥ brahma bhāga adhikā śilā tatas tu piṇḍikā kāryā pūrva uktaiḥ nāma lakṣaṇaiḥ tatas prakṣālitām kṛtvā pañcagavyena piṇḍikām devatā arcāḥ trayam mantram piṇḍikāsu niyojayet tatas utthāpya deveśam uttiṣṭha brahmaṇā iti ca ānīya garbhabhavanam pīṭa ante sthāpayet punar tatas muhūrtam viśramya ratna nyāsam samācaret puṣparāga indranīlam ca nīlam pūrva ādeḥ kramāt tālakam ca śilā vajram añjanam śyāmam eva ca kākṣī kāśī sa mākṣīkam gaurikam ca āditas kramāt nīvāram atha śyāmākam sarṣapam vrīhim eva ca agarut cādanam ca api uśīram ca tatas param vaiṣṇavīm sahadevīm ca lakṣmaṇām ca tatas param nyaset oṅkāra pūrvakam sarva bījāni dhātūn ca ratnāni oṣadhayaḥ tathā kāñcanam padmarāgam tu pāradam padmam eva ca kūrmam dharām vṛṣam tatra nyaset pūrva ādeḥ kramāt kanakam vidrumam tāmram kāṃsyam ca eva ārakūṭakam rajatam vimalam puṣpam loham ca eva krameṇa tu kāñcanam haritālam ca sarva abhāve api nikṣipet dadyāt bīja oṣadhi sthāne sahadevīm yavān api nyāsa mantrān atas vakṣye lokapāla ātmakān iha āgneyaḥ puruṣaḥ raktaḥ sarva deva mayaḥ śikhī yamaḥ ca utpala varṇa ābhaḥ kirīṭī sadā dharma sākṣī viśuddha ātmā tasmai nityam namaḥ namaḥ nirṛtiḥ tu pumān kṛṣṇaḥ sarva rakṣaḥ adhipaḥ mahān varuṇaḥ dhavalaḥ viṣṇuḥ puruṣaḥ nimnagādhipaḥ īśānapuruṣaḥ śuklaḥ sarva vidyā adhipaḥ mahān śūla hastaḥ virūpa akṣaḥ tasmai nityam namaḥ namaḥ padmayoniḥ caturmūrtiḥ rvedavāsāḥ pitāmahaḥ mantrāḥ tu sarva kāryyāṇām vṛddhi putra phala pradāḥ nyāsam kṛtvā tu mantrāṇām pāyasena anulepitam pāṭena ācchādayet śvabhram śuklena upari yatnataḥ tatas utthāpya deveśam iṣṭa deśe tu śobhane saṃsthāpayāmi deveśam devaḥ bhūtvā janārdanam gaṇeśam vṛṣa saṃsthañca sthāpayāmi trilocanam ṛṣibhiḥ saṃstutam devam caturvaktram jaṭā dharam pitāmaham mahā bāhum sthāpayāmi ambuja udbhavam sahasra kiraṇam śāntam apsaraḥ gaṇa saṃyuktam padma hastam mahā bāhum sthāpayāmi divākaram viṣṇoḥ tu vaiṣṇavān tadvat brāhmaṇān vai brahmaṇaḥ budhaiḥ saurāḥ sūryasya japtavyāḥ tathā anyeṣu tad āśrayāḥ veda mantra pratiṣṭā tu yasmāt ānanda dāyinī tasya pārśva sthitān anyān saṃsmaret parivāritaḥ gaṇam nandi mahākālam vṛṣa bhṛṅgi riṭim guham devīm vināyakam ca eva viṣṇum brahmāṇam eva ca rudram śakram jayantam ca lokapālān samantataḥ yasya siṃhāḥ rathe yuktāḥ vyāghra bhūtāḥ tathā uragāḥ ṛṣayaḥ lokapālāḥ ca devaḥ skandaḥ tathā vṛṣaḥ priyaḥ gaṇaḥ mātaraḥ ca somaḥ viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ nāgāḥ yakṣāḥ sa gandharvāḥ ye ca divyāḥ nabhaḥ carāḥ tam aham ṛkṣam īṃśānam śivam rudram umāpatim śāśvataḥ bhava pūjām me gṛhāṇa tvam namaḥ namaḥ om namaḥ svāgatam bhagavate namaḥ om namaḥ somāya sa gaṇāya sa parivārāya pratigṛhṇāt tatas puṇya aha ghoṣeṇa brahmaghoṣaiḥ ca puṣkalaiḥ sarvān ca etān pratiṣṭāsu mantrān japtvā punar punar catuḥ kṛtvā spṛśet adbhiḥ mūla madhye śirasi api dīna andha kṛpaṇān tadvat ye ca anye samupasthitāḥ saubhāgya śubha dam yasmāt lepanam vyādhi nāśanam tadvat pañcame api nivedayet ṣaṣṭe tu sa ghṛtam dadyāt candanam padma kesaram tasya lokapateḥ śāntiḥ deyān ca imāḥ ca dakṣiṇāḥ indrāya ābharaṇam dadyāt kāñcanam ca alpa vittavān agneḥ suvarṇam evasya ādyam asya mahiṣam tathā annam ca kāñcanam tat yān nairṛtam rākṣasam prati varuṇam pratimuktāni sa śuktīni pradāpayet somāya dhenuḥ dātavyā rajatam sa vṛṣam śive yasyām yasyām sañcalanam śāntiḥ syāt tatra tatra tu acalam kārayet tasmāt sikatābhiḥ sureśvaram annam vastram ca dātavyam puṇya aha jaya maṅgalam tris pañca sapta daśa vā dināni syāt mahotsavaḥ deva pratiṣṭā vidhiḥ eṣu tubhyam niveditaḥ pāpa vināśa hetoḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca arghasya api samāsena śrṛṇutvam vidhim uttamam dadhi akṣata kuśa agrāṇi kṣīram dūrvā tathā madhu gaja aśva rathyā valmīka varāha utkhāta maṇḍalām agni āgārāt tathā tīrthāt vrajāt go maṇḍalāt api kumbhe tu mṛttikām dadyāt uddhṛtā asi iti mantra vid śan naḥ devī iti apām mantram āpohiṣṭa iti vai tathā sāvitryā ādāya go mūtram gandha dvāra iti gomayam kuśa miśram kṣipet vidvān pañcagavyam bhavet tatas kuśa ambhasā tatas snānam devasya tvā iti kārayet tasya api ardhena vā kuryāt sa pādena śatena vā kāṃsyaiḥ vā pārthivaiḥ vā api snapanam śaktitas bhavet sahadevī vacā vyāghrī balā ca atibalā tathā mahauṣadhī aṣṭakam hi etat mahā snāneṣu yojayet priyaṅgavaḥ vrīhayaḥ ca snāneṣu parikalpitāḥ svastikam padmakam śaṅkham utpalam kamalam tathā śrīvatsam darpaṇan tadvat nandyāvartam atha aṣṭakam etāni gomayaiḥ kuryyāt mṛdā ca śubhayā tatas pañca varṇa ādikam tadvat pañca varṇam rajaḥ tathā dūrvā kṛṣṇa tilān dadyāt nīrājana vidhim tatas catvāriṃśat tatas dīpān dadyāt ca eva pradakṣiṇān tatas tu ābharaṇam dadyāt mahābhūṣāya te namaḥ caturbhiḥ aṣṭabhiḥ vā api dvābhyām ekena vā punar sa pañca ratna kalaśaiḥ sita vastra abhiveṣṭitaiḥ sita ambara dharaḥ snātvā devān saṃpūjya yatnatas yajña bhāṇḍāni sarvāṇi maṇḍapa upaskara ādikam pākhaṇḍinam yaḥ tu karoti bhaktyā vihāya viprān śruti dharma yuktān gurum pratiṣṭhā ādiṣu tatra nūnam kula kṣayaḥ syāt acirāt apūjyaḥ sthānam piśācaiḥ parigṛhyate vā apūjya tām yāti acireṇa śokaḥ vipraiḥ kṛtam yat śubha dam kule syāt prapūjyatām yāti ciram ca kālam Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ prāsādāḥ kīdṛśāḥ sūta kartavyāḥ bhūtim icchatā pramāṇam lakṣaṇam tadvat vada vistarataḥ adhunā sūtaḥ uvāca vāstau parīkṣite samyak vāstu deha vicakṣaṇaḥ vāstu upaśamanam kuryāt samidbhiḥ bali karmaṇā jīrṇa uddhāre tata udyāne tathā gṛha niveśane dvāra abhivartane tadvat prāsādeṣu gṛheṣu ca vāstu upaśamanam kuryāt pūrvam eva vicakṣaṇaḥ ekāśīti padam likhya vāstu madhye ca pṛṣṭhatas homaḥ tri mekhale kāryaḥ kuṇḍe hasta pramāṇake yavaiḥ kṛṣṇatilaiḥ tadvat samidbhiḥ kṣīravṛkṣakaiḥ pālāśaiḥ khādiraiḥ ca api madhu sarpi samanvitaiḥ īśa koṇe ghṛta annam tu śikhine vinivedayet jayāya ca dhvajān prītān paiṣṭam kūrmam ca sannyaset indrāya pañcaratnāni paiṣṭam ca kuliśam tathā vitānakam ca sūryāya dhūmram saktum tathā eva ca śaṣkulīḥ ca antarikṣāya dadyāt saktun ca vāyave lājāḥ pūṣṇe tu dātavyāḥ vitathe caṇaka audakam gṛhakṣata ayam adhvannam yamāya piśita odanam gandhaudanam ca gandharve bhṛṅgarājasya bhṛṃgikām mṛgāya yāvakam dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ kṛsarāma tā dauvārike dantakāṣṭham paiṣṭam kṛṣṇa balim tathā sugrīve puṣpakam dadyāt puṣpadantāya pāyasam kuśa stambena saṃyuktam tathā padmañca vāruṇam piṣṭam hiraṇmayam dadyāt āsurāya surā matā ghṛtaudanam ca śoṣāya yava annam pāpayakṣmaṇe ghṛta laḍḍukān tu rogāya nāge puṣpa phalāni tu bhagāya śālikam piṣṭam adityai dityai tu pūrikā dadyāt iti evam bāhyatas baliḥ sāvitre laḍḍukān dadyāt sa marīcam kuśa odanam savituḥ guḍa pūpān tu jayāya ghṛta candanam haritāla odanam dadyāt indrāya ghṛta saṃyutam āmam pakvam tathā māṃsam deyam syāt rājayakṣmaṇe pṛthvīdharāya māṃsāni kūṣmāṇḍāni ca dāpayet śarkarā pāyasam dadyāt aryamṇe punar eva hi pañcagavyam yavān śca eva tila akṣata mayam carum sarvebhyaḥ kāñcanam dadyāt brahmaṇe gām payasvinīm rākṣasīnām baliḥ deyaḥ api yādṛś yathā śruṇu māṃsodanam ghṛtam padma kesaram rudhira anvitam īśāna bhāgam āśritya carakyai vinivedayet pīta raktam balim dadyāt pūtanāyai sarakṣase vāyavyām pāpa rākṣasyai matsya māṃsam surā āsavam pāyasam ca api dātavyam sva nāmnā sarvatas kramāt dvijān bhaktyā ye ca anye gṛham āgatāḥ etat vāstu upaśamanam kṛtvā karma samārabhet nṛtya maṅgala vādyena kuryyāt brāhmaṇa vācanam jīvet varṣa śatam svarge kalpam ekam ca tiṣṭhati Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca āyataḥ syāt tribhiḥ bhāgaiḥ bhadra yuktaḥ su śobhanaḥ tathā anyantu pravakṣyāmi prāsādam liṅga mānataḥ liṅga pūjā pramāṇena kartavyā pīṭikā budhaiḥ piṇḍaka arddhe vibhāgaḥ syāt tad mānena tu bhittayaḥ śikharasya catur bhāgāt kartavyā ca pradakṣiṇā prāsādāt nirgatau kāryau kapālau garbha mānataḥ mañjaryām śca arddha bhāgena śukanāsām prakalpayet vedyāḥ ca upari yat śeṣam kaṇṭhaḥ ca amala sārakaḥ evam vibhajya prāsādam śobhanam kārayet budhaḥ garbha māna pramāṇena prāsādam śrṛṇu tat vijāḥ vibhajya navadhā garbham madhye syāt liṅga pīṭikā pāda aṣṭakam tu ruciram pārśvatas parikalpayet pādam pañcaguṇam kṛtvā bhittīnām ucchrayaḥ bhavet śuka nāsam prakurvīta tutīye vedikā matā śobhanaiḥ patra vallībhiḥ raṇḍakaiḥ ca vibhūṣitaḥ prāsādaḥ ayam tutīyaḥ tu mayā tubhyam niveditaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca sāmānyam aparam tadvat prāsādam śrṛṇu tat vijāḥ ratha aṅkaḥ tena mānena bhāga vinirgataḥ kārayet suṣiram tadvat prākārasya tri bhāgataḥ prāk grīvam pañca bhāgena niṣkāṣeṇa viśeṣataḥ kuryyāt āpañcabhāgena prāk grīve karṇa mūlataḥ liṅga māna anubhedena rūpa bhedena vā punar aṣṭa asraḥ ṣoḍaśa asraḥ ca vartulaḥ sarvabhadrakaḥ haṃsaḥ vṛṣaḥ suvarṇeśaḥ padmakaḥ atha samudgakaḥ prāsādāḥ nāmataḥ proktāḥ vibhāgam śrṛṇu tat vijāḥ śata śrṛṅgaḥ catur dvāraḥ bhūmikā ṣoḍaśa ucchritaḥ nānā vicitra śikhare meruḥ prāsādaḥ ucyate mandaraḥ dvādaśa proktaḥ kailāsaḥ nava bhūmikaḥ viṣāṇaka samāyuktaḥ nandanaḥ saḥ udāhṛtaḥ ṣoḍaśa asra samāyuktaḥ nānā rūpa samanvitaḥ citra śālā samopetaḥ vijñeyaḥ pañca bhūmikaḥ vṛṣasya ucchrāyataḥ tulyaḥ maṇḍalaḥ ca asra varjitaḥ siṃhaḥ siṃha ākṛtiḥ jñeyaḥ gajaḥ gaja samaḥ tathā aṅgalīpuṭa saṃsthānaḥ pañca aṇḍaka vibhūṣitaḥ ṣoḍaśa asraḥ samantāt ca vijñeyaḥ sa samudgakaḥ pārśvayoḥ candraśāle asya ucchrāyaḥ bhūmikā dvayam ṣoḍaśa asraḥ sa vijñeyaḥ vicitra śikharaḥ śubhaḥ prāk grīveṇa viśālena bhūmikāsu ṣaṣ unnataḥ anekaḥ candraśālaḥ ca gajaḥ prāsādaḥ iṣyate paryasta gṛha rājaḥ vai garuḍaḥ nāma nāmataḥ bhūmikā ṣaḍaśītiḥ tu bāhyatas sarvatas bhavet bhūmikā ṣoḍaśa asraḥ tu bhūmi dvayam atha adhikaḥ padma tulya pramāṇena śrīvṛkṣakaḥ iti smṛtaḥ pañca aṇḍakaḥ dvi bhūmiḥ ca garbhe hasta catuṣṭayam vṛṣaḥ bhavati nāmnā ayam prāsādaḥ sārvakāmikaḥ saptakāḥ pañcakāḥ ca eva prāsādāḥ vai mayā uditāḥ siṃha āsyena samājñeyāḥ ye ca anye tad pramāṇakāḥ candraśālaiḥ samopetāḥ sarve prāk grīva saṃyutāḥ aiṣṭakāḥ dāravāḥ ca eva śailāḥ vā syuḥ sa toraṇāḥ meruḥ pañcāśat hastaḥ syāt mandaraḥ pañca hīnakaḥ catvāriṃśat tu kailāsaḥ catustriṃśat vimānakaḥ vartulaḥ padmakaḥ ca eva viṃśat hastaḥ udāhṛtaḥ ete ṣoḍaśa hastāḥ syuḥ catvāraḥ deva vallabhāḥ kailāsaḥ mṛgarājaḥ ca vimāna chandakaḥ umataḥ ete dvādaśa hastāḥ syuḥ retaḥ iṣām iha mat matam garuḍaḥ aṣṭa karaḥ jñeyaḥ haṃsaḥ daśa udāhṛtaḥ evam ete pramāṇena kartavyāḥ śubha lakṣaṇāḥ yakṣa rākṣasa nāgānam mātṛ hastān praśaṃsyate tathā meru ādayaḥ sapta jyeṣṭha liṅge śubha āvahāḥ śrīvṛkṣaka ādayaḥ ca aṣṭau madhyamasya prakīrtitāḥ tathā haṃsa ādayaḥ pañca kanyase śubha dāḥ matāḥ valabhīcchandake gaurī jaṭā mukuṭa dhāriṇī gṛhe tu rakta mukuṭā utpala aṅkuśa dhāriṇī vara dā bhaya dā ca api pūjanīyā sa bhartṛkā tapaḥ vana sthām itarām tām tu saṃpūjayet budhaḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca maṇḍapa pravarān vakṣye prāsādasya anurūpataḥ vividhāḥ maṇḍapāḥ kāryāḥ jyeṣṭha madhya kanīyasaḥ śrīvatsaḥ vijayaḥ ca eva vāstukīrtiḥ śrutijayaḥ yajñabhadraḥ viśālaḥ ca suśliṣṭaḥ śatru mardanaḥ bhāgapañcaḥ nandanañca mānavaḥ mānabhadrakaḥ sugrīvaḥ haritaḥ ca eva kaṇikāraḥ śatarddhikaḥ saptaviṃśatiḥ ākhyātā lakṣaṇam śrṛṇuta dvijāḥ stambhāḥ yatra catuḥṣaṣṭi puṣpakaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ dviṣaṣṭi puṣpa bhadraḥ tu ṣaṣṭiḥ suvrataḥ ucyate kathyate mṛta nandanaḥ kauśalyaḥ ṣaṭcapañcāśat catuḥ pañcāśatā punar nāmnā tu buddhi saṃkīrṇaḥ dvihīnaḥ gajabhadrakaḥ jayāvahaḥ tu paścāśat śrīvatsaḥ tad vihīnakaḥ dvābhyām eva prahīyeta tatas śruti jayaḥ aparaḥ catvāriṃśat yajñabhadraḥ tad vihīnaḥ viśālakaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat ca eva suśliṣṭaḥ dvihīnaḥ śatrumarddanaḥ dvātriṃśat bhāga pañcaḥ tu triṃśadbhiḥ nandanaḥ smṛtaḥ aṣṭāviṃśat mānavaḥ tu mānabhadraḥ dvihīnakaḥ caturviṃśaḥ tu sugrīvaḥ dvāviṃśaḥ varṣaṇaḥ mataḥ viṃśatiḥ karṇikāraḥ syāt aṣṭādaśa śatardhikaḥ siṃhaḥ bhavet vihīnaḥ ca śyāmabhadraḥ dvihīnakaḥ maṇḍapāḥ kathitāḥ tubhyam yathāvat lakṣaṇa anvitāḥ evam tu śubha dāḥ proktāḥ ca anyathā tu aśubha āvahāḥ vistārāt dviguṇa ucchrāyam tat tri bhāgaḥ kaṭiḥ bhavet kaniṣṭha madhyamam jyeṣṭham yathāyogam prakalpayet aṅgulānām śatam sārddham catvāriṃśat tathā unnatam śatam ca aśīti sahitam vāta nirgamane bhavet adhikam daśabhiḥ tadvat tathā ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śatam śatamānam tṛtīyam ca navatyā aśītibhiḥ tathā daśa dvārāṇi ca etāni krameṇa uktāni sarvadā vṛkṣa koṇa brami dvāra stambha kūpa dhvajāt api kuḍya śvabhreṇa vā viddham dvāram na kṣayaḥ ca durgatiḥ ca eva pravāsaḥ kṣudh bhayam tathā daurbhāgyam bandhanam rogaḥ dāridyram kalaham tathā virodhaḥ ca artha nāśaḥ ca sarvam vedhāt bhavet kramāt pūrveṇa phalinaḥ vṛkṣāḥ kṣīra vṛkṣāḥ tu dakṣiṇe paścimena jalam śreṣṭham padma utpala vibhūṣitam uttare saralaiḥ tālaiḥ śubhā syāt puṣpa vāṭikā pārśvatas ca api karttavyam parivāra ādika ālayam yāmye tapovana sthānam uttare mātṛkāḥ gṛham vāruṇe śrīnivāsaḥ tu vāyavye gṛhamālikā uttare yajña śālā tu nirmālya sthānam uttare puratas vṛṣabha sthānam śeṣe syāt kusuma āyudhaḥ jalam vā api tathā aiśāne viṣṇuḥ tu jalaśāyī api evam āyatanam kuryāt kuṇḍa maṇḍapa saṃyutam ghaṇṭā vitānaka sa toraṇa citra yuktam yaḥ kārayet sura gṛham vividha dhvaja aṅkam śrīḥ tam na muñcati sadā divi pūjyate ca evam gṛha arcana vidhau api śaktitaḥ syāt Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ pūroḥ vaṃśaḥ tvayā sūta sabhaviṣyaḥ niveditaḥ sūrya vaṃśe nṛpāye tu bhaviṣyanti hi tān vada vaṃśa ante jñātayaḥ yāḥ ca rājyam prāpsyanti suvratāḥ brūhi saṃkṣepataḥ tāsām yathā bhāvyam anukramāt sūtaḥ uvāca sahadevāt ca bhavitā dhruvāśvaḥ vai mahā manāḥ kinnarāśvaḥ su nakṣatrāt bhaviṣyati parantapaḥ kinnara aśvāt antarikṣaḥ bhaviṣyati mahā manāḥ suṣeṇaḥ ca antarikṣāt ca sumitraḥ ca api amitra jit sumitrajaḥ bṛhadrājaḥ bṛhadrājasya vīryavān bṛhadvala anvavāye tu bhaviṣyāḥ kula varddhanāḥ iti evam mānavaḥ vaṃśaḥ prāk eva samudāhṛtaḥ somādhiḥ tasya dāyādaḥ rājā abhūt sa girivraje apratīpī ca ṣaṭtriṃśat samāḥ rājyam akārayat catvāriṃśat samāḥ tasya niramitraḥ divaṅgataḥ bṛhat karmā trayoviṃśat abdam rājyam akārayat senājit samprayātaḥ ca bhuktvā pañcaśatam mahīm aṣṭāviṃśati varṣāṇi mahīm prāpsyati vai vibhuḥ jayatām kṣatriyāṇām ca bālakaḥ pulakaḥ bhavet Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca miṣatām kṣatriyāṇām ca bālakaḥ pulaka udbhavaḥ aṣṭāviṃśati varṣāṇi pālakaḥ bhavitā nṛpaḥ kākavarṇaḥ sutaḥ tasya ṣaḍviṃśat prāpsyate mahīm ṣaṭtriṃśat ca eva varṣāṇi kṣemadhomāḥ bhaviṣyati caturviṃśat samāḥ saḥ api kṣemajit prāpsyate mahīm caturviṃśat tathā aikṣvākāḥ pāñcālāḥ saptaviśatiḥ kaliṅgāḥ ca eva dvātriṃśat aśmakāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ete sarve bhaviṣyanti eka kālam mahīkṣitaḥ uddhariṣyati kauṭilyaḥ samaiḥ dvādaśabhiḥ sutān bhavitā śatadhanvā ca tasya putraḥ tu ṣaṣ samāḥ bhavitā nava varṣāṇi tasya putraḥ ca saptatiḥ iti ete śuṅga bhṛtyāḥ tu smṛtā kāṇvāyanāḥ nṛpāḥ catvāriṃśat dvijāḥ hi ete kāṇvāḥ bokṣyanti vai mahīm catvāriṃśat pañca ca eva bhokṣyanti imām vasundharām ete praṇata sāmantāḥ bhaviṣyāḥ dhārmikāḥ ca ye Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca śuṅgānām ca eva yat śeṣam kṣapitvā tu balīyasaḥ pūrṇa utsaṃgaḥ tatas rājā varṣāṇi aṣṭādaśa eva tu daśa ca aṣṭau ca varṣāṇi tasya lambodaraḥ sutaḥ svātiḥ ca bhavitā rājā samāḥ tu aṣṭādaśa eva tu kuntalaḥ svātikarṇaḥ tu bhavitā aṣṭau samāḥ nṛpaḥ eka saṃvatsaram rājā svātivarṇaḥ bhaviṣyati pañca mandulakaḥ rājā bhaviṣyati samāḥ nṛpa cakoraḥ svātikarṇaḥ tu ṣaṣ māsān vai bhaviṣyati aṣṭāviṃśati sutaḥ tasya sulomā vai bhaviṣyati śivaśrīḥ vai sulomāt tu saptā eva bhavitā nṛpaḥ śivaskandha śāntikarṇāt bhavitā hi ātmajaḥ samāḥ sulomā sapta varṣāṇi anyaḥ teṣām bhaviṣyati teṣām varṣa śatāni syuḥ catvāri ṣaṣṭiḥ eva ca āndhrāṇām saṃsthitāḥ rājye teṣām bhṛtya anvaye nṛpāḥ sapta gardabhilāḥ ca api śakāḥ ca aṣṭādaśa eva tu trayodaśa guruṃḍāḥ ca hūṇā hi ekonaviṃśātiḥ yavana aṣṭau bhaviṣyanti sapta aśīti mahīm imām bhokṣyanti imām vasundharām śatāni trīṇi aśītiṃca śatāni aṣṭādaśa eva tu śatāni arddhañcatuṣkāṇi bhavitavyāḥ trayodaśa āndhrāḥ śrīpārvvatīyāḥ ca te dvipañcāśatam samāḥ sarve āryyāḥ mlecchāḥ ca sarvatas saptarṣīṇām upari e tat smṛtam vai divya saṃjñayā krameṇa eva ca dṛśyante sva varṇa antara dāyakam pratipannam kali yugam pramāṇam tasya me śrṛṇu catuḥ varṣāṇām vai smṛtam budhaiḥ catvāri aṣṭa sahasrāṇi saṅkhyātam mānuṣeṇa tu devāpi putraḥ satyaḥ tu ailānām bhavitā nṛpaḥ kṣīṇe kali yuge ca eva tiṣṭhanti iti kṛte yuge etena krama yogena ailāḥ ikṣvākavaḥ nṛpāḥ iṣṭavantaḥ ca te sarve sarve niyuta dakṣiṇāḥ teṣām te nidhana utpattau loka saṃsthitayaḥ sthitāḥ ā ete deva gaṇaiḥ sārddham śiṣṭāḥ ye tān nibodhita avaśiṣṭāḥ yuga ākhyāḥ te tatas vaivasvataḥ hi ayam etat vaḥ rājarṣayaḥ ye tu atītāḥ te yugaiḥ saha āyuḥ kīrtim dhanam svargam putravān ca abhijāyate Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ nyāyena arjanam arthānām varddhanam ca abhirakṣaṇam sūtaḥ uvāca yat tat ṣoḍaśadā proktam vāsudevena bhū tale sarva dānānām tulā puruṣa saṃjñakam hiraṇyagarbha dānam ca brahmāṇḍam tad anantaram pañca lāṅgalakam tadvat dharā dānam tathā eva ca mahābhūta ghaṭaḥ tadvat ṣoḍaśam parikīrtitam sarvāṇi etāni kṛtavān purā śamvarasūdanaḥ vāsudevaḥ tu bhagavān ambarīṣaḥ atha bhārgavaḥ kārtavīryya arjunaḥ nāma prahlādaḥ pṛthuḥ eva ca kuryuḥ anye mahīpālāḥ kecid ca bharata ādayaḥ eṣām anyatamam kuryyāt vāsudeva prasādataḥ mahādāna mukham kuryyāt vipraiḥ ca eva anumoditaḥ etat eva āha manave paripṛṣṭaḥ janārdanaḥ tulāpuruṣa yāgaḥ ayam yeṣām ādau vidhīyate yuga ādiṣu parāgeṣu tathā manvantara ādiṣu saṅkrāntau tau vaidhṛti dine caturdaśī aṣṭamīṣu ca dravya brāhmaṇa lābhe vā śraddhā vā yatra jāyate tīrthe vā āyatane goṣṭe kūpa ārāma saritsu ca gṛhe vā āyatane vā api taḍāge rucire tathā puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam ṣoḍaśa aratni mātram tu daśa dvādaśa vākarān tad madhye toraṇam kuryyāt sāradāru mayam budhaḥ samekhalā yoni yutāni kuryāt sampūrṇakumbhāni sahāsanāni su tāmra pātra dvaya saṃyutāni sa yajña pātrāṇi su viṣṭarāṇi hasta pramāṇāni tila ājya dhūpa puṣpa upahārāṇi su śobhanāni atra arcanam brahma śiva acyutānām tatra eva kāryyam phala mālya vastraiḥ lokeśa varṇāḥ paritas patākāḥ madhye dhvajaḥ kiṅkiṇikā yutaḥ syāt dvāreṣu kāryāṇi ca toraṇāni catvāri api kṣīra vanaspatīnām śālā iṅgudī candana devadāru śrīparṇi bilva priya kāñcana uttham stambha dvayam hasta yuga avakhātam kṛtvā dṛḍham pañcakara ucchritam ca samāna jātiḥ ca tulā avalambhyā haimena madhye puruṣeṇa yuktā dairghyeṇa sā hasta catuṣṭayam syāt pṛthu tvam asyāḥ tu daśa aṃgulāni suvarṇa paṭṭa ābharaṇā tu kāryā sā loha pāśa dvaya śrṛṅgalābhiḥ yutā suvarṇena tu ratna mālā vibhūṣitā a mālya vilepanābhyām cakram likhet vārija garbha yuktam nānā rajobhiḥ bhuvi puṣpa kīrṇam vimānakam ca upari pañca varṇam saṃsthāpayet puṣpa phala upaśobham atha ṛtvijaḥ veda vidaḥ ca kāryyāḥ su rūpa veśa anvaya śīla yuktāḥ vidhāna dakṣāḥ paṭavaḥ anukūlāḥ ye ca āryya deśa prabhavāḥ dvijendrāḥ guruḥ ca vedāntavidāryya vaṃśa samudbhavaḥ śīla kula abhirūpaḥ purāṇa śāstra abhirataḥ ati dakṣaḥ prasanna gambhīra sarasvatīkaḥ sita ambaraḥ kuṇḍala hemasūtra keyūra kaṇṭa ābharaṇa abhirāmaḥ pūrveṇa ṛgveda vidau atha āstām yajuḥ vidau dakṣiṇatas ca śastau vināyaka ādi graha lokapāla vasu aṣṭaka āditya marut gaṇānām brahmācyute sa arkam vanaspatīnām sva mantrataḥ homa catuṣṭayam syāt japyāni sūktāni tathā eva ca eṣām anukrameṇa api yathā svarūpam homa avasāne kṛta tūryya nādaḥ guruḥ gṛhītvā bali puṣpa dhūpam śubha aśubha ānanda śucām adhīśa śivāya naḥ pāhi makham namaḥ te japeyuḥ śāntikādhyāyam jāpakāḥ sarvatodiśam punar pradakṣiṇām kṛtvā tulām ārohayet budhaḥ na ciram dhārayet gehe suvarṇam prokṣitam budhaḥ śīghram para svīkaraṇāt śreyaḥ prāpnoti mānavaḥ karma kṣayāt iha punarbhuvi rājarājaḥ bhūpāla mauli maṇiḥ añcita pāda pīṭhaḥ yaḥ vā śrṛṇoti paṭhati indra samāna rūpaḥ prāpnoti dhāma sa purandara deva juṣṭam Purāṇa puṇyam dinam atha āsādya tulā puruṣa dāna vat kuryyāt upoṣitaḥ tadvat lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ puṇya aha vācanam kṛtvā tadvat kṛtvā adhivāsanam brāhmaṇaiḥ ānayet kumbham tapanīya mayam śubham tri bhāga hīna vistāram ājya kṣīra abhipūritam daśa astrāṇi ca ratnāni dātrīm sūcīm tathā eva ca hema nālam sa piṭhakam bahis āditya saṃyutam tathā eva āvaraṇam nābheḥ upavītam ca kāñcanam padma ākāram pidhānam syāt samantāt aṃgula adhikam brahma ādayaḥ tathā devāḥ namaḥ te viśva dhāriṇe evam āmantrya tad madhyam āviśya āste udak mukhaḥ muṣṭibhyām parisaṃgṛhya dharmarāja caturmukhau jānu madhye śiraḥ kṛtvā tiṣṭet ucchvāsa pañcakam gīta maṅgala ghoṣeṇa guruḥ utthāpayet tatas caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ bhūyas tatas te dvija puṅgavāḥ grāmam vā viṣayam vā api yat anyat api sambhavet pureṣu lokapālānām pratimanvantaram vaset matim api ca janānām yaḥ dadāti priya artham vibudha pati janānām nāyakaḥ syāt amogham Purāṇa puṇyam dinam atha āsādya tulā puruṣa dāna vat lokeśa āvāhanam kuryyāt adhivāsanakam tathā kalaśa dvaya saṃyuktam brahmāṇḍam kāñcanam budhaḥ pūrveṇa ananta śayanam pradyumnam pūrvadakṣiṇe prakṛtim dakṣiṇe deśe saṅkarṣaṇam atas param samantāt guḍapīṭha sthān arcayet kāñcanān budhaḥ sthāpayet vastra samvītān pūrṇa kumbhān daśa eva tu daśa eva dhenavaḥ deyāḥ saha eva ambara dohanāḥ bhakṣya bhojya anna dī phala mālya anulepanaiḥ homa adhivāsana ante ca snāpitaḥ veda puṅgavaiḥ saptarṣi loka amara bhū tala īśa garbheṇa sārdham vitara abhirakṣām ye duḥkhitāḥ te sukhinaḥ bhavantu prayāntu pāpāni carācarāṇām tvad dāna śastra ahata pātakānām brahmāṇḍa doṣaḥ pralayam vrajantu brahmāṇḍa dāna śakalīkṛta pātaka ogham ānandayet ca jananī kulam api aśeṣam iti paṭhati śruṇoti vā yaḥ etat sura bhavaneṣu gṛheṣu dhārmikāṇām Purāṇa puṇyam dinam athā āsādya tulāpuruṣa dāna vat puṇya aha vācanam kṛtvā lokeśa āvāhanam tathā kāñcanam kārayet budham nānā phala samanvitam nānā vihaga vacśrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca kārayet śakti tāñcipalākū dhvamā sahasram prakalpayet mandāram dakṣiṇe bhāge śriyām sārdham ghṛta upari paścime pāribhedam tu sāviḍyā saha jairuke surabhī saṃyutam tat valiṣṭheṣu haricandanam tūrīya aṃśena kurvīta saumyena phala saṃyutam phala mālya yutam tadvat upariṣṭāt vitānakam homa adhivāsana ante ca snāpitaḥ veda puṃgavaiḥ saṃtāna ādyaiḥ upetaḥ san pāhi saṃsāra sāgarāt stūyamānaḥ divaḥ putra pautra śra pautrakaiḥ divi kalpa śatam tiṣṭhet vājarājaḥ bhavet tatas nārāyaṇa kathā saktaḥ nārāyaṇa puram vrajet yaḥ vā paṭhet sakala kalpataru upadānam yaḥ vā śṛṇoti puruṣaḥ alpa dhanaḥ smaret vā Purāṇa puṇyām tithim samāsādya yuga manvantara ādikīm puṇya aha vācanam kuryāt homaḥ kāryaḥ tathā eva ca go sahasram bahis kuryāt vastra mālya vibhūṣaṇam suvarṇa śrṛṅga ābharaṇam raupya pāda samanvitam suvarṇa tilaka upetam hema paṭṭaiḥ alaṅkṛtam gavām daśaka madhye syāt kāñcanaḥ nandikeśvaraḥ lavaṇat roma śikhare mālayā ikṣu phala saṃyutaḥ kuryāt pala śatāt ūrdhvam sarvam etat aśeṣatas go śate api daśa aṃśena sarvam etat samācaret payaḥ vrataḥ punar tiṣṭet eka aham go sahasra daḥ tad dine brahmacārī syāt yadi icchet vipulām śriyam śatam eka uttaram tadvat pitṝṇām tārayet budhaḥ mātāmahānām tadvat ca putra pautra samanvitaḥ rājarājaḥ bhavet punar anubhavati mudam vā mucyamānaḥ nikāmam Purāṇa lokeśa āvāhanam tadvat homaḥ kāryaḥ adhivāsanam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt kuṇḍa maṇḍapa vedikam kanīyasī tad ardhena kāmadhenuḥ prakīrtitāḥ vedyām kṛṣṇājinam nyasya guḍa prastha samanvitam nyaset upari tām dhenum mahā ratnaiḥ alaṅkṛtām ikṣu daṇḍa aṣṭakam tadvat nānā phala samanvitam bhājanam ca āsanam tadvat tāmra dohanakam tathā kauśeya vastra dvaya saṃyuta aṅgām dīpa ātapatra ābharaṇa abhirāmām sa cāmarām kuṇḍalinī sa ghaṇṭām suvarṇa śrṛṅgīm parirūpya pādām rasaiḥ ca sarvaiḥ paritas abhijuṣṭām haridrayā puṣpa phalaiḥ anekaiḥ snātaḥ tatas maṅgala veda ghoṣaiḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sa puṣpa hastaḥ āvāhayet tām guruṇā ukta mantraiḥ dvijāya dadyāt atha darbha pāṇiḥ tvad dāna śastra śakalīkṛta pāpaka oghaḥ prāptaḥ asmi nirvṛttim atīva parām namāmi saṃsāra duḥkha śamanāya yatasva kāmam tvām kāmadhenum iti deva gaṇāḥ vadanti prāpnoti dhāma sa purandara deva juṣṭam kanyā gaṇaiḥ parivṛtaḥ padam indumauleḥ Purāṇa puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam pūrṇa kumbha aṣṭaka upetam mālya ikṣu phala saṃyutam śayyām sa upaskarām tadvat hema mārtaṇḍa saṃyutām tatas sarva oṣadhī snāna snāpitaḥ dvija puṅgavaiḥ sarvam śayyā ādikam da tvā bhuñjīta ā tailam eva hi imam hiraṇya aśva vidhim karoti yaḥ saṃpūjyamānaḥ divi deva saṅghaiḥ Purāṇa mālya ikṣu phala saṃyuktam puruṣeṇa samanvitam ābhārāt tri palāt ūrdhvam śaktitas kārayet budhaḥ aśva aṣṭakena saṃyuktam caturbhiḥ atha vājibhiḥ puṇya kālam atha avāpya pūrvavat snāpitau dvijaiḥ dhāmnām adhīśāya divākarāya pāpa ogha dāva anala dehi śāntim vasu aṣṭakā āditya marut gaṇānām tvam eva dhātā paramam nidhānam dedīpyamāna vapuṣām vijita prabhāvam ākramya maṇḍalam akhaṇḍita caṇḍabhānoḥ Purāṇa puṇyām tithim atha āsādya tulā dāna vat vipra vācanakam kuryāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ kuryāt puṣparatha ākāram kāñcanam maṇi maṇḍitam nānā phala sāma āyuktam upariṣṭāt vitānakam kauśeyam amlāna kusuma anvitam caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ sārddham gaubhiḥ aṣṭābhiḥ anvitam svarūpataḥ karibhyām ca yuktam kṛtvā nivedayet tathā maṅgala śabdena snāpitaḥ veda puṅgavaiḥ vidyādhara amara muni indra gaṇa abhijuṣṭam prāpnoti asau padam atīndriyam indumauleḥ nayati sa pitṛ putrān bāndhavān api aśeṣān kṛta gaja ratha dānāt śāśvatam sadma viṣṇoḥ Purāṇa bhūmi dānam naraḥ dadyāt pañca lāṅgalaka anvitam sāra dāru mayān kṛtvā halān pañca vicakṣaṇaḥ sarva upakaraṇaiḥ yuktān anyān pañca ca kāñcanān vṛṣān lakṣaṇa saṃyuktān daśa ca eva dhurandharān rūpya pāda agra tilakān rakta kauśeya bhūṣaṇān sragdāma candana yutān śālāyām adhivāsayet tulāpuruṣa vat kuryyāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ āhūya dvija dāmpatyam hemasūtra aṃgulīyakaiḥ dhurandhara aṅge tiṣṭhanti tasmāt bhaktiḥ śive astu me mānena anena yaḥ dadyāt nivartana śatam budhaḥ vidhinā anena tasya āśu kṣīyate pāpa saṃhatiḥ yāvanti lāṅgalaka mārga mukhāni bhūmeḥ bhāsāṃpateḥ duhituḥ aṅgaja romakāṇi saṃpūjyate pitṛ pitāmaha bandu yuktaḥ śambhoḥ padam vrajati ca amaranāyakaḥ san Purāṇa pāpa kṣaya karam nṛṇām amaṅgalya vināśanam nadīnada samopetām sapta sāgara veṣṭitām mahā ratna sabhā ākīrṇām vasuradrāka saṃyutām śata trayeṇa vā kuryāt viśatena śatena vā kuryāt pañca palāt rurdhvam aśaktaḥ api vicakṣaṇaḥ tulāpuruṣa vat kuryāt vihoktā vāhanam budhaḥ vedyām kṛṣṇa arjinam kṛtvā tilān upari vinyaset iti evam kārayitvā tām adhivāsana pūrvakam tvam eva lakṣmīḥ govinde śive gaurī iti ca āsthitā gāyatrī brahmaṇaḥ pārśve jyotsnā candre ravī prabhā buddhiḥ bṛhaspatī hi atā medhā muniṣu saṃsthitā viśvam vyāpya sthitā yasmāt tatas viśvaṃbharā smṛtā śeṣam ca eva atha ṛtvigbhyaḥ prāṇi patya visarjayet iti paṭhati yaḥ ittham yaḥ śṛṇoti prasaṅgāt api kaluṣa vitānaiḥ mukta dehaḥ samantāt Purāṇa tapanīyasya śuddhasya viṣuva ādiṣu kārayet viśvacakram tatas kuryāt kṛṣṇājina tila upari mālya ikṣu phala ratnāni vitānam ca api kārayet āyudhan ca api vāsaḥ ca bhavāt uddhara mām atas Purāṇa puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ cāmīkara mayīḥ kuryāt daśa kalpalatāḥ samāḥ vidyādhara suparṇānām mithunaiḥ upaśobhitāḥ hārān āditsubhiḥ siddhaiḥ phalāni ca vihaṅgamaiḥ lokapāla anukāriṇyaḥ kartavyāḥ tāsu devatāḥ ibha āsana sthā tu guḍe pūrvatas kuliśa āyudhā rajanī saṃsthitā agnāyī śruva pāṇiḥ atha anale yāmye ca mahiṣa ārūḍhā gadinī taṇḍula upari ghṛte tu nairṛtī sthāpyā sa khaṅgā dakṣiṇa apare vāruṇe vāruṇī kṣīre jhaṣa sthā nāga pāśinī patākinī ca vāyavye mṛga sthā śarkarā upari saumyā tileṣu saṃsthāpyā śaṅkhinī nidhi saṃsthitā maulinyaḥ vara dā tadvat kartavyāḥ bālaka anvitāḥ śaktyā pañca palāt ūrdhvam ā sahasrāt prakalpayet sarvāsām upari sthāpyam pañca varṇam vitānakam namaḥ namaḥ pāpa vināśinībhyaḥ brahmāṇḍa loka īśvara pālinībhyaḥ āśaṃsita ādhikya phala pradābhyaḥ digbhyaḥ tathā kalpalatā vadhūbhyaḥ Purāṇa tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ tatas vāruṇa homa ante snāpitaḥ veda puṅgavaiḥ kṣīra udaka ājya dadhi mādhura lāvaṇa ikṣu sāra amṛtena bhuvanatraya jīva saṅghān pāpa kṣaya amṛta vilepana bhūṣaṇāya lokasya bibhrati tat astu mama api lakṣmīḥ Purāṇa puṇyam dinam atha āsādya tulā puruṣa dāna vat lokeśa āvāhanam kṛtvā tatas dhenum prakalpayet bhūmau kṛṣṇājinam kṛtvā lavaṇa droṇa saṃyutam puccha agre cāmaram dadyāt samīpe tāmra dohanam tathā dhānyāni sarvāṇi pādāḥ ca ikṣu mayāḥ smṛtāḥ evam viracanam kṛtvā tadvat homa adhivāsanam ṛtvigbhyaḥ dakṣiṇām dadyāt dhenum āmantrayet tatas tvām sarva deva gaṇa dhāma yatas paṭhanti rudra indra sūrya kamala āsana vāsudevāḥ yaḥ puṇyam āpya dina matra kṛta upavāsaḥ pāpaiḥ vimukta tanuḥ eti padam murāreḥ Purāṇa puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt lokeśa āvāhana ādikam varāheṇa uddhṛtām tadvat kuryāt pṛthvīm sa paṅkajām varuṇam ca āsana gatam kāñcanam makara upari hutāśanam meṣa gatam vāyum kṛṣṇa mṛgāsanam tathā kośa adhipam kuryāt mūṣika stham vināyakam sāmavedasya vīṇā āsyāt veṇum dakṣiṇatas nyaset paritas sarva dhānyāni cāmara āsana darpaṇam śayyān ca jala kumbhān ca pañca varṇam vitānakam mahābhūta adhidevebhyaḥ śāntiḥ astu śivam mama brahmāṇḍe sarva bhūteṣu tasmāt śrīḥ akṣayā astu me ṣoḍaśa etāni yaḥ kuryāt mahā dānāni mānavaḥ iha paṭhati yaḥ ittham vāsudevasya pārśve sa suta pitṛ kalatraḥ saṃśrṛṇoti iha samyak Purāṇa idānīm kalpa nāmāni samāsāt kathaya acyuta rauravaḥ pañcamaḥ proktaḥ ṣaṣṭaḥ devaḥ iti smṛtaḥ sadyaḥ atha navamaḥ proktaḥ īśānaḥ daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ tama ekādaśaḥ proktaḥ tathā sārasvataḥ paraḥ trayodaśaḥ udānaḥ tu gāruḍaḥ atha caturdaśaḥ kaurmaḥ pañcadaśaḥ proktaḥ paurṇamāsyām ajāyata ṣoḍaśaḥ nārasiṃhaḥ tu samānaḥ tu tatas aparaḥ āgneyaḥ aṣṭādaśaḥ proktaḥ soma kalpaḥ tathā aparaḥ mānavaḥ viṃśatiḥ proktaḥ tat pumān iti ca aparaḥ vaikuṇṭhaḥ ca aparaḥ tadvat lakṣmī kalpaḥ tathā aparaḥ caturviṃśatimaḥ proktaḥ sāvitrī kalpa saṃjñakaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ tatas ghoraḥ vārāhaḥ tu tatas aparaḥ pitṛkalpaḥ tathā ante tu yā kuhūḥ brahmaṇaḥ parā ādau eva hi māhātmyam yasmin yasya vidhīyate saṅkīrṇāḥ tāmasāḥ ca eva rājasāḥ sātvikāḥ tathā rajaḥ tamaḥ mayāḥ tadvat ete triṃśat udāhṛtāḥ saṅkīrṇeṣu sarasvatyāḥ pitṛṇām vyuṣṭiḥ ucyate agneḥ śivasya māhātmyam tāmaseṣu divākare rājaseṣu ca māhātmyam adhikam brahmaṇaḥ smṛtam yasmin kalpe tu yat proktam purāṇam brahmaṇā purā yaḥ tu dadyāt imān kṛtvā haimān parvaṇi parvaṇi brahma viṣṇu pure vāsam munibhiḥ pūjyate divi muni rūpān tatas kṛtvā dadyāt kalpān vicakṣaṇaḥ purāṇa saṃhitā ca iyam tava bhūpa mayā uditā sarva pāpa harā nityam ārogya śrī phala pradā brahma saṃvatsara śatāt eka aham śaivam ucyate śiva varṣa śatāt ekam nimeṣam vaiṣṇavam viduḥ yadā sa viṣṇuḥ jāgarti tadā idam ceṣṭate jagat iti uktvā devadeveśaḥ matsya rūpī janārdanaḥ sva antaram pālayāmāsa mārtaṇḍa kula varddhanaḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca sāṃkhyam śārīrakam proktam caturmukha mukha udbhavam manvantarāṇām uddeśaḥ vainya rāja abhivarṇanam kārtavīryasya mahātmyam vṛṣṇi vaṃśa anukīrtanam bhṛguśāpaḥ tathā viṣṇoḥ daityaśāpaḥ tathā eva ca vajrāṅgasya tu saṃbhūtiḥ tāraka utpattiḥ eva ca kalpa anukīrtanam tadvat grantha anukramaṇīm tathā tulā dāna ādi bahuśas mahā dāna anukīrtanam kalpa anukīrtanam tadvat granta anukramaṇīm tathā etat pavitram kalyāṇam mahā pāpa haram śubham asmāt purāṇāt api pādam ekam paṭet tu yaḥ saḥ api vimukta pāpaḥ nārāyaṇa ākhyam padam eti nūnam anaṅga vat divya sukhāni bhuṅkte
